## 1. Abschnitt. Sarga und Pratisarga  (A. 17.6b-17; Br. 1.31-56; H. 27-53; Śidh. 51.3-28.)

śṛṇudhvaṃ tāṃ kathāṃ divyāṃ sarvapāpapramocinīṃ |
kathyamānāṃ mayā citrāṃ bahvarthāṃ śrutivistarāṃ. || 1 ||
yaś cemāṃ dhārayen nityaṃ śṛṇuyād vāpy abhīkṣṇaśaḥ, |
svavaṃśadhāraṇaṃ kṛtvā svargaloke mahīyate. || 2 ||
avyaktaṃ kāraṇaṃ yat, tan nityaṃ sadasadātmakaṃ |
pradhānaṃ, puruṣas tasmān nirmame viśvam īśvaraḥ. || 3 ||
taṃ budhyadhvaṃ muniśreṣṭhā brahmāṇaṃ amitaujasaṃ |
sraṣṭāraṃ sarvabhūtānāṃ nārāyaṇaparāyaṇaṃ. || 4 ||
ahaṃkāras tu mahatas,
tasmād bhutāni jajñire,
bhūtabhedāś ca bhūtebhya,
iti sargaḥ sanātanaḥ. || 5 ||
vistarāvayavaṃ caiva
yathāprajñāṃ yathāśruti |
kīrtyamānaṃ śṛṇudhvaṃ vai
pūrveṣāṃ kīrtivardhanaṃ! || 6 ||
dhanyaṃ yaśasyaṃ śatrughnaṃ
svargyam āyuhpravardhanaṃ |
kīrtanaṃ sthirakīrtīnāṃ
sarveṣāṃ puṇyakarmaṇāṃ. |
yasmāt kalpāyate kalpaḥ samagraṃ śucaye śucih, || 7 ||
a vṛṣṇivamśad vaksyāmi
bhūtasargam anuttamaṃ || 8 ||
tasmai hiraṇyagarbhāya
puruṣāyeśvarāya ca |
ajāya sthāṇurūpāya
variṣṭhāya prajābhuve |
namaskṛtya pravakṣyāmi
bhuvaḥ sargam anuttamaṃ. || 8 ||
brahmā sraṣṭā, hariḥ pātā
saṃhartā ca maheśvarah, |
tasya sargasya nānyo 'sti
kāle kāle yathā gate. || 9 ||
tataḥ svayambhūr bhagavān sisrkṣur vividhāḥ prajāḥ |
apā eva sasarjādau, tāsu vīryam avāsrjat. || 10 ||
āpo nārā iti proktā, āpo vai narasūnavah, |
ayanaṃ tasya tāḥ pūrvaṃ, tena nārāyaṇah smṛtah. || 11 ||
hiraṇyavarṇam abhavat tad aṇḍam udakeśayaṃ, |
tatra jajñe svayaṃ brahmā svayambhūr iti nah śrutaṃ. || 12 ||
hiraṇyagarbho bhagavān uṣitvā parivatsaraṃ |
tad aṇḍam akarod dvaidhaṃ, divaṃ bhuvam athāpi ca. || 13 ||
[adho 'thordhvaṃ prayuktāni
bhuvanāni caturdaśa. |]
tayoḥ śakalayor madhya ākāśam asrjat prabhuh, |
apsu pāriplavāṃ prthvīṃ, diśāś ca daśadhā dadhe. || 14 ||
tatra kālaṃ mano vācaṃ kāmaṃ krodham atho ratiṃ |
sasarja sṛṣṭiṃ tadrūpāṃ
sraṣṭum icchan prajāpatiḥ. || 15 ||
marīcim atry-aṅgirasau pulastyaṃ pulahaṃ kratuṃ |
vasiṣṭhaṃ ca mahātejāḥ so 'sṛjat sapta mānasān. || 16 ||
sapta brahmāṇa ity ete purāṇe niścayaṃ gatāḥ |
nārāyaṇātmakānāṃ vai
saptānāṃ brahmajanmanāṃ. || 17 ||
tato 'sṛjat punar brahmā rudraṃ roṣātmasaṃbhavaṃ |
sanatkumāraṃ ca vibhuṃ pūrveṣām api pūrvajaṃ. || 18 ||
saptaite janayanti sma prajā, rudraś ca bho dvijāḥ |
ataḥ sanatkumārāś ca tejāḥ saṃkṣipya tiṣṭhataḥ. || 19 ||
teṣāṃ sapta mahāvaṃśā divyā devagaṇānvitāḥ |
kriyāvantaḥ prajāvanto
maharṣibhir alaṃkṛṭāḥ. || 20 ||
vidyuto 'śanimeghāṃś ca rohitendradhanūṃṣi ca |
vayāṃsi ca sasarjādau parjanyaṃ ca sasarja ha. || 21 ||
ṛco yajūṃṣi sāmāni nirmame yajñasiddhaye, |
śādhyās tair ayajan devān, ity evam anuṣūsuma. || 22 ||
mukhād devān ajanayat
pitṝṃś caivātha vakṣasaḥ |
prajanāc ca manuṣyān vai,
jaghaṇān nirmame 'suran. || 22 ||
uccāvacāni bhūtāni gātrebhyas tasya jajñire |
āpavasya prajāsargaṃ srjato hi prajāpateḥ. || 23 ||
srjyamānāḥ prajā naiva vivardhante yadā, tadā |
dvidhā kṛtvātmano deham ardhena puruṣo 'bhavat, || 24 ||
ardhena nārī, tasyāṃ sa sasrje vividhāḥ prajāḥ; |
divaṃ ca pṛthivīṃ caiva mahimnā vyāpya tiṣṭhati. || 25 ||
virājam asrjad viṣṇuḥ, so 'srjat puruṣaṃ virāt, |
puruṣaṃ taṃ manuṃ vidyād, tasya manvantaraṃ smṛtaṃ. || 26 ||
dviṭīyaṃ mānasasyaivaṃ manor antaram ucyate. |
sa vairājāḥ prajāsargaṃ sasarja puruṣaḥ prabhuh. || 27 ||
nārāyaṇavisargasya prajās tasyāpy ayonijāḥ. |
āyuṣmān kīrtimān dhanyāḥ prajāvāmś ca bhaven naraḥ, || 28 ||
ādisargaṃ viditvemaṃ yatheṣtāṃ prāpnuyād gatiṃ. || 29 ||

## 2. Abschnitt. Vaṃśa | 1. Kapitel

samśrṣṭāsu prajāsv evam āpavo vai prajāpatiḥ |
lebhe vai puruṣaḥ patnīṃ śatarūpām ayonijāṃ. || 1 ||
āpavasya mahimnā tu divam āvṛtya tiṣṭhataḥ |
dharmeṇaiva muniśreṣṭhāḥ śatarūpā vyajāyata. || 1 ||
sā tu varṣaśataṃ taptvā tapaḥ paramaduścaraṃ |
bhartāraṃ dīptatapasaṃ puruṣaṃ pratyapadyata. || 1 ||
sa vai svāyambhuvo viprāḥ puruṣo manur ucyate. |
tasyaikasaptatiyugaṃ manvantaram ihocyate. || 1 ||
vairājāt puruṣād vīraṃ
śatarūpā vyajāyata, |
priyavrat-ottānapādau
vīrāt kāmyā vyajāyata. || 1 ||
kāmyā nāma sutā śreṣṭhā
kardamasya prajāpateḥ, |
sūta uvāca:
cākṣuṣasya nisargaṃ tu
samāsāc chrotum arhatha |
yasyānvavāye saṃbhūtaḥ
pṛthur vainyaḥ pratāpavān |
prajānāṃ patayaś cānye
dakṣaḥ prācetasas tathā. || 1 ||
kāmyāputrāś ca catvāraḥ:
samrāṭ kukṣīr virāṭ prabhuḥ. || 16 ||
uttānapādaṃ jagrāha putram atriḥ prajāpatiḥ. |
dattakaḥ sa tu putro 'sya
rājā hy āsīt prajāpatiḥ, || 21 ||
svāyaṃbhuvena manunā
datto 'treḥ kāraṇaṃ prati. |
manvantaram athāsādyā
bhaviṣyac cākṣuṣasya ha || 22 ||
ṣaṣṭhaṃ tad anuvakṣyāmi
upodghātena vai dvijāḥ. |
uttānapādāc caturaḥ sūnṛtā suṣuve sutān || 2 ||
dharmasya kanyā suśroṇī sūnṛtā nāma viśrutā, |
utpannā vājimedhena, dhruvasya jananī śubhā. || 3 ||
dharmasya patnyāṃ lakṣmyāṃ vai
utpannā sā śucismitā. |
dhruvaṃ ca kīrtimantaṃ ca ayuṣmantaṃ vasuṃ tathā |
uttānapādo 'janayat
uttānapādo 'janayat
sūnṛtāyāṃ prajāpatiḥ. || 4 ||
kanye dve ca śucismite: || 4 ||
dhruvo varṣasahasrāṇi trīṇi divyāni vīryavān |
tapas tepe nirāhāraḥ, prārthayan sumahad yaśaḥ. || 5 ||
tasmai brahmā dadau prītaḥ
sthānam ātmasamaṃ prabhuḥ, |
acalaṃ caiva purataḥ
saptarṣīṇāṃ prajāpatiḥ. || 6 ||
tasyābhimānaṃ rḍdhiṃ ca mahimānaṃ nirīkṣya ca |
devāsurāṇām ācāryaḥ ślokaṃ apy uśanā jagau: || 7 ||
aho 'sya tapaso vīryaṃ, aho śrutam, aho 'dbhutaṃ, |
yam adya purataḥ kṛṭvā dhruvaṃ saptarṣayaḥ sthitāḥ! || 8 ||
tasmāc chīṣṭiṃ ca bhavyaṃ ca
dhruvāc chambhur vyajāyata. |
śiṣṭer ādhatta succhāyā
pañca putrān akalmaṣān: || 9 ||
ripuṃ ripumjayaṃ vipraṃ
vṛkalaṃ vṛkatejasaṃ |
ripor ādhatta bṛhatī cākṣuṣaṃ
ajījanat puṣkariṇyāṃ vairaṇyāṃ cākṣuṣo manuṃ |
prajāpater ātmajāyāṃ vīraṇasya mahātmanaḥ. || 11 ||
manor ajāyanta daśa naḍvalāyāṃ mahaujasaḥ |
kaṇyāyāṃ sumahāvīryā vairājasya prajāpateḥ: || 12 ||
ūruḥ pūruḥ śatadyumnas tapasvī śatyavāk kavīḥ |
agniṣṭud atirātraś ca śudyumnaś ceti te nava || 13 ||
abhimanyuś ca daśamo naḍvalāyāṃ mahaujasaḥ. |
ūror ajanayat putrān śaḍāgneyī mahāprabhān: || 14 ||
aṅgaṃ śumanasaṃ śvātiṃ kratum aṅgirasaṃ gayaṃ. |
aṅgāt śunīthāpatyaṃ vai venam ekaṃ vyajāyata. || 15 ||
apacāreṇa venasya prakopaḥ sumahān abhūt, |
prajārtham ṛṣayo 'thāsyā mamanthur dakṣiṇaṃ karaṃ. || 16 ||
venasya mathite pāṇau saṃbabhūva mahān nṛpaḥ. |
taṃ dṛṣṭvā munayaḥ prāhur : | vainyo nāma mahīpālo,
eṣa vai muditāḥ prajāḥ || 17 || yaḥ pṛthuḥ parikīrtitāḥ, || 17 ||
kariṣyati mahāteja
yaśāś ca prāpsyate mahat. |
sa dhanvī kavacī jātas, tejasā nirdahan iva. || 18 ||
pṛthur vainyas tadā cemāṃ rarakṣa kṣatrapūrvajaḥ, |
rājasūyābhiśiktānām ādyaḥ sa vasudhādhipaḥ. || 19 ||
tasmāc caiva samutpannau nipuṇau sūta-māgadhau. |
teneyaṃ gaur mahārājñā dugdhā sasyāni dhīmatā || 20 ||
prajānāṃ vṛttikāmena devaiḥ sarṣigaṇaiḥ saha |
pitṛbhir dānavaiś caiva gandharvaiḥ sāpsarogaṇaiḥ || 21 ||
sarpaiḥ puṇyajanaiś caiva vīrudbhiḥ parvatais tathā; |
teṣu teṣu ca pātreṣu duhyamānā vasuṃdharā || 22 ||
prādād yathepsitaṃ kṣīraṃ, tena prāṇān adhārayan. |
pṛthos tu putrau dharmajñau jajñāte 'ntardhi-pālīnau. || 23 ||
śikhaṇḍini havirdhānam antardhānād vyajāyata, |
havirdhānāt ṣaḍāgneyī dhiṣaṇājanayat sutān: || 24 ||
prācīnabarhiṣaṃ śukraṃ gayaṃ kṛṣṇaṃ vraj-ājinau. |
prācīnabarhir bhagavān mahān āsīt prajāpatiḥ || 25 ||
havirdhānān mahārājo,
yena saṃvardhitāḥ prajāḥ. |
prācināgrāḥ kuśās tasya
prthivyāṃ dvijasattamāḥ || 26 ||
prācinabarbir bhagavān
prthivītalacāriṇāḥ. |
balaśrutatapoviryaiḥ
pṛthivyāṃ ekaṛāḍ asau. |
prācīnāgrāḥ kuśās tasya
tasmāt prācīnabarhy asau. || 26 ||
samudratanayāyāṃ tu kṛtadāro 'bhavat prabhuḥ || 27 ||
mahatas tapasaḥ pāre savarṇāyāṃ prajāpatiḥ. |
savarṇādhatta sāmudrī daśa prācīnabarhiṣaḥ, || 28 ||
sarvān pracetaso nāma dhanurvedasya pāragān. |
aprthagdharmacaraṇās te 'tapyanta mahat tapaḥ || 29 ||
daśa varṣasahasrāṇi samudrasalileśayāḥ. |
tapaś caratsu prthivīṃ pracetaḥsu mahīruhāḥ || 30 ||
arakṣya'māṇām āvavrur, babhūvātha prajākṣayaḥ. |
pratyāhrte tadā tasmin cākṣuṣasyāntare manoḥ || 31 ||
nāśakan māruto vātuṃ, vṛtaṃ kham abhavad drumaiḥ; |
daśa varṣasahasrāṇi na śekuś ceṣṭituṃ prajāḥ. || 32 ||
tad upāśrutya tapasā yuktāḥ sarve pracetasaḥ |
mukhebhyo vāyum agniṃ ca sasrjur jātamanyavaḥ. || 33 ||
unmūlān atha tān vṛkṣān kṛtvā vāyur aśoṣayat, |
tān agnir adahad ghora, evam āsīd drumakṣayaḥ. || 34 ||
druma'kṣayam atho buddhvā kim̄cicchiṣṭeṣu śākhiṣu |
upagamyābravīd etān rājā somaḥ prajāpatīn: || 35 ||
kopaṃ yacchata rājānaḥ sarve prācīnabarhiṣaḥ, |
vṛkṣaśūnyā kṛtā pṛthvī,
śāmyetām agnimārutau! || 36 ||
yuṣmākaṃ tejaso 'rdhena mama cārdhena tejasah |
asyām utpatsyate vidvān dakṣo nāma prajāpatih. || 39 ||
sa imāṃ dagdhabhūyiṣṭhāṃ yuṣmattejomayena vai |
agnināgnisamo bhūyah prajāḥ samvardhayiṣyati. || 40 ||
tataḥ somasya vacanāj jagṛhus te pracetasah |
samhṛtya kopaṃ vrkṣebhyah patniṃ dharmeṇa māriṣāṃ. || 41 ||
māriṣāyāṃ tatas te vai manasā garbham ādadhuh. |
daśabhyas tu pracetobhyo māriṣāyāṃ prajāpatiḥ || 42 ||
dakṣo jajñe mahātejāḥ somasyāṃśena vīryavān. |
putrān utpādayāmāsa | asrjan manasā tvādau
somavaṃśavivardhanān. || 43 || prajā dakṣo na maithunāt. || 43 ||
acarāṃś ca carāṃś caiva dvipado 'tha catuṣpadaḥ. |
sa srṣṭvā manasā dakṣaḥ paścād asrjata striyaḥ. || 44 ||
dadau sa daśa dharmāya, kaśyapāya trayodaśa, |
śiṣṭāḥ somāya rājñe ca | kālasya nayane yuktāḥ
nakṣatrākhyā dadau prabhuḥ. || saptaviṃśatim indave. || 45 ||
ebhyo dattvā tato 'nyā vai catasro 'riṣṭanemine, |
dve caiva bahuputrāya dve caiv-āṅgirase tathā, |
kaṇyām ekāṃ kṛśāśvāya, tebhyo 'patyaṃ nibodhata! || 45 ||
antaraṃ cākṣuṣasyātra manoḥ ṣaṣṭhaṃ tu gīyate |
manor vaivasvatasyāpi saptamasya prajāpateḥ. || 45 ||
tāsu devāḥ khagā gāvo nāgā ditija-dānavāḥ |
gandharvāpsarasaś caiva jajñire 'nyāś ca jātayaḥ. || 46 ||
tataḥ prabhṛti loke 'smin prajā maithunasaṃbhavāḥ, |
saṃkalpād darśanāt sparśāt pūrveṣāṃ srṣṭir ucyate. || 47 ||
munaya (ṛṣaya) ūcuḥ:
devānāṃ dānavānāṃ ca gandharvoragarakṣasāṃ |
saṃbhavaḥ kathitaḥ pūrvaṃ dakṣasya ca mahātmanaḥ. || 48 ||
aṅguṣṭhād brahmaṇo jāto | prāṇāt prajāpater janma
dakṣaḥ proktas tvayānagha | dakṣasya kathitam̃ tvayā. || 49 ||
vāmāṅguṣṭhāt tathā caiva
tasya patnī vyajāyata. || 49 ||
kathaṃ prācetasatvaṃ sa punar lebhe mahātapāh̃, |
etaṃ naḥ saṃśayaṃ sūta vyākhyātuṃ tvam̃ ihārhasi, |
dauhitraś caiva somasya kathaṃ śvaśuratāṃ gataḥ? || 50 ||
(lomaharṣaṇa) sūta uvāca:
utpattiś ca nirodhaś ca nityaṃ bhūteṣu vartate, |
ṛṣayo 'tra na muhyanti vidyāvantaś ca ye janāḥ. || 51 ||
yuge yuge bhavanty ete sarve dakṣādayo nṛpaḥ |
punaś caiva nirudhyante, vidvāṃs tatra na muhyati. || 52 ||
jyaiṣṭhyaṃ kāniṣṭhyaṃ apy eṣāṃ pūrvaṃ nāsīḍ dvijottamaḥ |
tapa eva garīyo 'bhūt prabhāvaś caiva kāraṇam̃. || 53 ||
imāṃ visṛṣṭiṃ dakṣasya
yo vidyāt saccarācarāṃ, |
prajāvān) āyur uttīrṇaḥ) svargaloke mahīyate. || 54 ||
evaṃ) sargaḥ samākhyātaś cākṣuṣasya samāsataḥ |
ity ete ṣaḍ visargā hi ) krāntā manvantarātmakāḥ || 541 ||
svāyaṃbhuvādyāḥ saṃkṣepāc cākṣuṣāntā yathākramaṃ. |
ete sargā yathā prājñaiḥ) proktā vai) dvijasattamāḥ || 542 ||
vaivasvatavisargeṇa) teṣāṃ jñeyas tu vistaraḥ. |
anyūnā nātiriktās te) sarve sargā vivasvataḥ. || 543 ||
ārogvāyuḥ pramāṇena) dharmataḥ kāmato 'rthataḥ |
etān eva guṇān eti, yaḥ paṭhaty) anasūyakāḥ. || 544 ||
vaivasvatasya vakṣyāmi sāṃpratasya mahātmanāḥ |
samāsavyāsataḥ sargaṃ bruvato me nibodhata! || 545 ||

## 2. Abschnitt. Vaṃśa | 2. Kapitel

munaya (ṛṣaya) ūcuḥ:
devānāṃ dānavānāṃ ca gandharvoragarakṣasāṃ |
utpattiṃ vistareṇaiva mama brahman prakīrtaya! || 1 ||
lomaharṣaṇa (sūta) uvāca:
prajāḥ sṛjeti vyādiṣṭaḥ pūrvaṃ dakṣaḥ, svayaṃbhuvā |
yathā sasarja bhūtāni,
tathā śṛṇuta bho dvijāḥ! || 2 ||
mānasāny eva bhūtāni pūrvam evāsṛjat prabhuḥ: |
ṛṣīn devān sagandharvān asurān atha rākṣasān || 3 ||
yakṣabhūtapiśācāṃś ca vayaḥpaśumṛgāṃs tathā. |
yadāsyā manasā sṛṣṭā na vyavardhanta tāḥ prajāḥ, || 4 ||
tadā saṃcintya dharmaṃtmā
prajāhetoḥ prajāpatiḥ. |
sa maithunena dharmeṇa sisrkṣur vividhāḥ prajāḥ, || 5 ||
asiknīm āvahat patnīṃ vīraṇasya prajāpateḥ |
sutāṃ, sutapasā yuktāṃ mahatīṃ lokadbāriṇīṃ. || 6 ||
atha putrasahasrāṇi
vairaṇyāṃ pañca vīryavān |
asiknyāṃ janayāmāsa dakṣa eva prajāpatiḥ. || 7 ||
tāms tu drṣṭvā mahābhāgān samvivardhayiṣūn prajāh |
devarṣiḥ priyasamvādo nāradaḥ prābravīd idaṃ || 8 ||
nāśāya vacanaṃ teṣāṃ śāpāyaivātmanas tathā, |
yaṃ kaśyapaḥ sutavaraṃ parameṣṭhī vyajījanat. || 9 ||
dakṣasya vai duhitari | mānasaḥ kaśyapasyāsīd
dakṣaśāpabhayān muniḥ | dakṣaśāpavaśāt punaḥ |
| tasmāt sa kaśyapasyātha
| dviṭīyo mānaso 'bhavat |
pūrvaṃ sa hi samutpanno nāradaḥ parameṣṭhinah. || 10 ||
asiknyām atha vairanyāṃ
bhūyo devarṣisattamah |
taṃ bhūyo janayāmāsa
piteva munipuṃgavaṃ. || 10 ||
tena dakṣasya vai putrā haryaśvā iti viśrutāḥ |
dharmāṛthaṃ nāśitāḥ sarve vidhinā ca na samśayah. || 11 ||
tasyodyatas tadā dakṣo | tasyodyatas tadā dakṣaḥ
nāśāyāmitavikramah | kruddho nāśāya vai prabhuḥ |
brahmārṣīn purataḥ kṛtvā yācitaḥ parameṣṭhinā. 12 ||
tato 'bhisamḍhiṃ cakre vai dakṣas tu parameṣṭhinā: |
kanyāyāṃ nārado mahyaṃ tava putro bhaved iti. || 13 ||
tato dakṣaḥ sutāṃ prādāt priyāṃ vai parameṣṭhine, |
tasmāt sa nārado jajñe bhūyaḥ śāpabhayād rṣiḥ. || 14 ||
munaya (rṣaya) ūcuh:
kathaṃ vināśītāḥ putrā nāradena maharṣiṇā |
prajāpatisutās te vai śrotum icchāma tattvataḥ. || 15 ||
lomaharṣaṇa (sūta) uvāca:
dakṣasya putrā haryaśvā vivardhayiṣavaḥ prajāḥ |
samāgatā mahāvīryā, nāradas tān uvāca ha: || 16 ||
bāliśā bata yūyaṃ vai,
nāsyā jānītha vai bhuvaḥ |
pramāṇaṃ sraṣṭukāmā vai
prajāḥ prācetasātmajāḥ, || 17 ||
antar ūrdhvam adhaś caiva kathaṃ srakṣyatha vai prajāḥ? |
te tu tadvacanaṃ śrutvā prayātāḥ sarvatodiśaṃ; || 18 ||
adyāpi na nivartante samudrebhya ivāpagāḥ. |
haryaśveṣv atha naṣṭeṣu dakṣaḥ prācetasāḥ punaḥ || 19 ||
vairaṇyāṃ atha putrāṇāṃ sahasram asrjat prabhuḥ. |
vivardhayiṣavas te tu
śabalāśvās tathā prajāḥ || 20 ||
prajā vivardhayiṣavaḥ
śabalāśvāḥ punas tu te || 20 ||
pūrvoktaṃ vacanaṃ te tu
nāradena pracoditāḥ. |
anyonyam ūcus te sarve: samyag āha mahān ṛṣiḥ; || 21 ||
bhrātṝṇāṃ padavī caiva gantavyā nātra samśayah! |
jñātvā pramāṇaṃ prṭhvyāś ca sukhaṃ srakṣyāmahe prajāḥ. || 22 ||
| [ekāgrāḥ svasthamanasā yathāvad anuśāsitāḥ ]
te 'pi tenaiva mārgeṇa prayātāḥ sarvatodiśaṃ, |
adyāpi na nivartante samudrebhya ivāpagāḥ. || 23 ||
tataḥ prabhṛti vai bhrātā bhrātur anveṣaṇe rataḥ |
prayāto naśyati kṣipraṃ, tan na kāryaṃ vipaścitā || 24 ||
naṣṭeṣu śabalāśveṣu dakṣaḥ kruddho 'śapad vibhuḥ |
nāradam: nāśam ehīti garbhavāsaṃ vaseti ca! || 25 ||
tāmś caiva naṣṭān vijñāya
putrān dakṣaḥ prajāpatih |
ṣaṣṭiṃ dakṣo 'ṣrjat kanya vairaṇyāṃ, iti naḥ śrutaṃ. || 26 ||
tās tadā pratijagrāha bhāryārthaṃ kaśyapaḥ prabhuḥ |
dharmaḥ somaś ca bhāgavāṃs tathaivanye maharṣayaḥ. || 27 ||
dadau sa daśa dharmāya, kaśyapāya trayodaśa, |
saptaviṃśati somāya, catasro 'riṣṭanemine, || 28 ||
dve caiva bahuputrāya, dve caiv-aṅgirase tathā, |
dve kṛśāśvāya viduṣe, tāsāṃ nāmāni me śṛṇu: || 29 ||
| imāṃ viṣṛṣṭiṃ dakṣasya kṛtsnāṃ yo veda tattvataḥ, |
| āyuṣmān kīrtimān dhanyaḥ prajāvāṃś ca bhavaty uta. || 29 ||
arundhatī vasur yāmī lambā bhānur marutvatī |
saṃkalpā ca muhūrtā ca sādhyā viśvā tathaiva ca, || 30 ||
dharmapatnyo daśa tv etās, tāsv apatyāni bodhata: |
viśvedevās tu viśvāyāḥ, sādhyā sādhyān vyajāyata, || 31 ||
marutvatyā marutvanto, vasos tu vasavaḥ sutāḥ, |
bhānos tu bhānavaḥ putrā, muhūrtās tu muhūrtajāḥ, || 32 ||
lambāyāś caiva ghoṣo 'tha, nāgavīthī ca yāmijā, |
pṛthivīviṣayaṃ sarvam arundhatyāṃ vyajāyata, || 33 ||
samkalpāyās tu sarvātmā jajñe samkalpa eva hi. |
nāgavīthyāṃ ca yāminyāṃ vṛṣalāś ca vyajāyata. || 34 ||
parā yāḥ somapatnīś ca dakṣaḥ prācetaso dadau, |
sarvā nakṣatranāmnyas tā jyotiṣe parikīrtitāḥ. || 34 ||
ye tv anye khyātimanto vai devā jyotiḥpurogamāḥ, |
vasavo 'ṣṭau samākhyāṭās, teṣāṃ vakṣyāmi vistaram: || 35 ||
āpo dhruvaś ca somaś ca dharaś caiv-anilo 'nalaḥ |
pratyūṣaś ca prabhāsaś ca vasavo nāmabhiḥ smṛtāḥ || 36 ||
apasya putro vaitaṇḍyaḥ śramaḥ śānto munis tathā; |
dhruvasya putro bhagavān kālo lokaprakālanaḥ. || 37 ||
somasya bhagavān varcā, varcasvī yena jāyate; |
dharasya putro draviṇo hutahavyavahas tathā. || 38 ||
manoharāyaḥ śīśiraḥ prāṇo 'tha ramaṇas tathā. |
anilasya śivā bhāryā, tasyāḥ putro manojavāḥ || 39 ||
avijñātagatiś caiva dvau putrāv anilasya ca. |
agniputraḥ kumāras tu śarastambhe vyajāyata, || 40 ||
tasya śākho viśākhaś ca naigameyaś ca prṣṭhataḥ, |
apatyaṃ kṛttikānāṃ tu kārttikeya iti smṛtaḥ. || 41 ||
skandah sanatkumāraś ca srṣṭaḥ pādena tejasaḥ. |
pratyūṣasya viduḥ putraṃ rṣiṃ nāmnāṭha devalaṃ. || 42 ||
dvau putrau devalasyāpi kṣamāvantau manīṣiṇau; |
brhaspates tu bhaginī varastrī brahmacāriṇī || 43 ||
yogasiddhā jagat kṛtsnam asaktā vicacāra ha; |
prabhāsasya tu sā bhāryā vasūnām aṣṭamasya ca, || 44 ||
viśvakarmā mahābhāgo yasyāṃ jajñe prajāpatiḥ, |
kartā śilpasahasrāṇāṃ tridaśānāṃ ca vardhakiḥ, || 45 ||
bhūṣaṇānāṃ ca sarveṣāṃ kartā, śilpavatāṃ varaḥ, |
yaḥ sarveṣāṃ vimānāni daivatānāṃ cakāra ha, || 46 ||
manuṣyāś copajīvanti yasya śilpaṃ mahātmanaḥ. |
surabhī kāśyapād rudrān ekādaśa vinirmame || 47 ||
| mahādevaprasādena, tapasā bhāvitā satī: |
ajaikapād ahirbudhnyas tvaṣṭā rudraś ca vīryavān. || 48 ||
tvaṣṭuś caivatmajaḥ śrīmān) viśvarūpo mahāyaśāḥ) |
haraś ca bahurūpaś ca tryambakaś cāparājitāḥ || 49 ||
vṛṣākapiś ca śambhuś ca kapardī raivatas tathā |
mṛgavyādhaś ca śarvaś) ca kapālī ca dvijottamāḥ). || 50 ||
ekādaśaite kathita) rudrās tribhuvaneśvarāḥ. |
śataṃ tv evaṃ samākhyātaṃ rudrāṇām amitaujasām). || 51 ||
| purāṇe muniśārdūlā) yair vyāptaṃ sacarācaram). |
| dārān śṛṇudhvaṃ viprendrāḥ kaśyapasya prajāpateḥ)! || 51 ||
aditir ditir danuś caiva) ariṣṭā surasā khaśā) |
surabhir vinatā caiva tāmrā krodhavaśā irā) || 52 ||
kadrūr) muniś) ca bho viprās), tās) apatyāni bodhata)! |
pūrvamanvanṭare śreṣṭhā) dvādaśāsan surottamāḥ || 53 ||
tuṣitā nāma, te 'nyonyam ūcur vaivasvate 'ntare |
upasthite 'tiyaśasaś) cākṣuṣasyāntare) manoḥ: || 54 ||
hitārthaṃ sarvalokānām) samāgamya parasparaṃ |
āgacchata drutaṃ devā), aditiṃ sampraviśya vai) 55 ||
manvantare prasūyāmas, tan naḥ) śreyo bhaviṣyati). |
evam uktvā) tu te sarve cākṣuṣasyāntare manoḥ || 56 ||
mārīcāt kaśyapāj jātās tv adityā dakṣakanyayā. |
tatra viṣṇuś ca śakraś ca jajñāte punar eva hi, || 57 ||
aryamā caiva dhātā ca tvaṣṭā pūṣā tathaiva ca |
vivasvān savitā caiva mitro varuṇa eva ca || 58 ||
aṃśo bhagaś cātitejā ādityā dvādaśa smṛtāḥ. |
cākṣuṣasyāntare pūrvam āsan ye tuṣitāḥ surāḥ, || 59 ||
vaivasvate 'ntare te vai ādityā dvādaśa smṛtāḥ. |
saptaviṃśati yāḥ proktāḥ somapatnyo mahāvratāḥ, || 60 ||
tāsām apatyāny abhavan dīptāny amitatejasaḥ. |
ariṣṭanemipatnīnām apatyānīha ṣoḍaśa: || 61 ||
bahuputrasya viduṣaś catasro vidyutāḥ smṛtāḥ, |
prātyaṅgirasajāḥ śreṣṭhāṛco brahmārṣisatkṛtāḥ. || 62 ||
kṛśāśvasya tu devarṣer devapraharaṇāḥ smṛtāḥ, |
ete yugasahasrānte jāyante punar eva hi. || 63 ||
sarve devagaṇāś cātra trayastriṃśat tu kāmajāḥ, |
teṣām api ca bho viprā nirodhotpattir ucyate. || 64 ||
yathā sūryasya gagana udayāstamayāv iba, |
evaṃ devanikāyās te saṃbhavanti yuge yuge. || 65 ||
dityāḥ putradvayaṃ jajñe kaśyapād, iti naḥ śrutam: |
hiraṇyakaśipuś caiva hiraṇyākṣaś ca vīryavān, || 66 ||
siṃhikā cābhavat kanyā, vipracitteḥ parigrahaḥ, |
| saimhikeyā iti khyātā yasyāḥ putrā mahābalāḥ. || 67 ||
hiraṇyakaśipoḥ putrāś catvāraḥ prathitaujasah: |
anuhrādaś ca hrādaś ca prahrādaś caiva vīryavān, || 68 ||
saṃhrādaś ca caturtho 'bhūd. hrādaputro hrādas tathā, |
saṃhrādaputraḥ sundaś ca nisundaś caiva tāv ubhau. |
hradasya putra āyuṣmāñ śibir bāṣkala eva ca. || 69 ||
virocanaś ca prāhrādir, balir jajñe virocanāt. |
baleḥ putraśataṃ tv āsīd bāṇajyeṣṭhaṃ tapodhanāḥ: || 70 ||
dhṛtarāṣṭraś ca sūryaś ca candramāś c-endratāpanaḥ |
kumbhanābho gardabhākṣaḥ kukṣir, ityevamādayaḥ. || 71 ||
bāṇas teṣām atibalo jyeṣṭhaḥ paśupateḥ priyaḥ. |
purākalpe tu bāṇena prasādy-omāpatiṃ prabhūṃ |
pāṛśvato vihariṣyāmi, ity evaṃ yācito varāḥ. || 71 ||
hiraṇyākṣasutāḥ pañca vidvāṃsaḥ su mahābalāḥ: |
utkaraḥ śakuniś caiva bhūtasaṃṭāpanas tathā || 72 ||
mahānābhaś ca vikrāntaḥ kālanābhas tathaiva ca. |
abhavan danuputrāś ca śataṃ tīvraparākrāmāḥ || 73 ||
tapasvino mahāvīryāḥ, prādhānyena bravīmi tāṇ: |
dvimūrdhā śāṅkuniś caiva tathā śāṅkuśirāḥ prabhuḥ || 74 ||
śāṅkukarṇo virādhaś ca gaveṣṭhī dundubhis tathā, |
ayomukhaḥ śambaraś ca kapilo vāmanas tathā, || 75 ||
mārīcir maghavāṃś caiva irā gargaśirā vṛkaḥ, |
vikṣobhaṇaś ca ketuś ca ketuvīrya-śatahradau, || 76 ||
indrajit sarvajic caiva vajranābhas tathaiva ca, |
ekacakro mahābāhus tārakaś ca mahābalāḥ, || 77 ||
vaiśvānaraḥ pulomā ca vidrāvaṇa-mahāśirāḥ, |
svarbhānur vṛṣaparvā ca tuhuṇḍaś ca mahāsurāḥ, || 78 ||
sūkṣmaś caiva nicandraś ca ūrṇanābho mahāgirīḥ, |
asilomā sukeśaś ca śaṭhaś ca balako madaḥ, || 79 ||
tathā gaganamūrdhā ca kumbhanābho mahasurah, |
pramado mayaś ca kupatho hayagrīvaś ca vīryavān, || 80 ||
vaiṣṛpaḥ sa-virūpākṣaḥ supatho 'tha harāharau, |
hiranyakaśipuś caiva śatamāyaś ca śambaraḥ, || 81 ||
śarabhaḥ śalabhaś caiva vipracittiś ca vīryavān; |
sarva ete danoḥ putrāḥ kaśyapād abhijajñire. || 82 ||
| vipracittipradhānās te dānavāḥ sumahābalāḥ, |
eteṣāṃ yad apatyaṃ tu, na tac chakyaṃ dvijottamāḥ |
prasaṃkhyātuṃ bahutvāc ca putrapautram anantakaṃ. || 82 ||
svarbhānos tu prabhā kanyā, pulomnas tu śacī sutā |
upadānavī hayaśirāḥ, śarmiṣṭhā vārṣaparvaṇī. || 83 ||
pulomā kālikā caiva vaiśvānarasute ubhe, |
bahvapatye mahāvīrye mārīces tu parigrahaḥ. || 84 ||
tayoḥ putrasahasrāṇi ṣaṣṭir dānavanandanāḥ. |
caturdaśa śatāny anyān hiranyapuravāsinaḥ || 85 ||
mārīcir janayāmāsa mahatā tapasānvitaḥ. |
paulomāḥ kālakeyāś ca dānavās te mahābalāḥ. || 86 ||
avadhyā devatānāṃ hi hiraṇyapuravāsinaḥ |
pitāmahaprasādena, ye hatāḥ savyasācinā. || 87 ||
prabbāyā nahuṣaḥ putro, jayantaś ca śacīsutaḥ |
puruṃ jajñe 'tha śarmiṣṭhā, duṣmantam upadānavī. || 88 ||
tato 'pare mahāvīryā dānavās tv atidāruṇāḥ |
siṃhikāyām athotpannā vipracitteḥ sutās tathā, || 89 ||
daitya-dānavasaṃyogāj jātās tīvraparākramāḥ |
saiṃhikeyā iti khyātās trayodaśa mahābalāḥ: || 90 ||
vyaṃśaḥ śalyaś ca balinau nalaś caiva mahābalāḥ |
vātāpir namuciś caiva ilvalaḥ svasṛmas tathā, || 91 ||
añjiko narakaś caiva kālanābhas tathaiva ca, |
rāhur jyeṣṭhas tu tesāṃ vai sūryacandrapramardanaḥ, |
saramānas tathā caiva svarakalpaś ca vīryavān, || 92 ||
mūkaś caiva tuhuṇḍaś ca hradaputrau babhūvatuḥ, |
mārīcaḥ sundaputraś ca tāḍakāyāṃ vyajāyata, || 93 ||
ete vai dānavāḥ śreṣṭhā danuvaṃśavivardhanāḥ; |
teṣāṃ putrāś ca pautrāś ca śataśo 'tha sahasraśaḥ. || 94 ||
saṃhrādasya tu daityasya nivātakavacāḥ kule |
samutpannāḥ sumahatā tapasā bhāvitātmanaḥ. || 95 ||
tisrah koṭyāḥ sutās teṣāṃ maṇivatyāṃ nivāsināḥ, |
avadhyās te 'pi devānām arjunena nipātitāḥ. || 96 ||
ṣaṭ sutāḥ sumaḥāsattvās tāmrāyāḥ parikīrtitāḥ: |
kakī śyenī ca bhāsī ca sugrīvī śucir gṛdhrikā. || 97 ||
kakī kākān ajanayad, ulūkapratyulūkakān, |
śyenī śyenāms tathā bhāsī bhāsān gṛdhrāmś ca gṛdhry api, || 98 ||
śucir audakān pakṣigaṇān, sugrīvī tu vyajāyata |
aśvān uṣṭrān gardabhāmś ca. tāmrāvamśāḥ prakīrtitāḥ. || 99 ||
vinatāyās tu putrau dvau aruṇo garuḍas tathā. |
suparṇāḥ patatāṃ śreṣṭho, dāruṇāḥ svena karmaṇā. || 100 ||
surasāyāḥ sahasraṃ tu sarpāṇām amitaujasāṃ |
anekaśirasāṃ teṣāṃ khecarāṇāṃ mahātmanāṃ. || 101 ||
kādraveyās tu balināḥ sahasraṃ amitaujasāḥ |
suparṇavaśagā nāgā jajñire 'neṣkamastakāḥ, || 102 ||
yeṣāṃ pradhānāḥ satatam: śeṣa-vāsuki-takṣakāḥ, |
airavato mahāpadmaḥ kambal-āśvatarāv ubhau || 103 ||
elāpatras tathā padmaḥ karkoṭaka-dhanamjayau, |
mahānīla-mahākarṇau dhṛtarāṣṭra-balāhakau, || 104 ||
kuharaḥ puṣpadamṣṭraś ca durmukhaḥ sumukhas tathā, |
| śāṅkhaś ca śaṅkhapālaś ca kapilo vāmanas tathā, |
nahuṣaḥ śāṅkharomā ca maṇir ityevamādayaḥ. || 105 ||
| teṣāṃ putrāś ca pautrāś ca garuḍena nipātitāḥ |
| caturdaśa sahasrāṇi krūrāṇām anilāśināṃ. || 105 ||
gaṇaṃ krodhavaśaṃ vidḍhi, tasya sarve ca daṃṣṭriṇaḥ, |
sthalajāḥ pakṣiṇo 'bjāś ca dharāyāḥ prasavāḥ smṛtāḥ. || 106 ||
gāṣ tu vai janayāmāsa surabhir mahiṣāṃṣ tathā, |
irā vṛkṣalatāvallīs tṛṇajātīś ca sarvaśaḥ. || 107 ||
khaśā tu yakṣarakṣāṃṣi, munir apsarasas tathā, |
ariṣṭā tu mahāsattvān gandharvān samajījanat. || 108 ||
ete kaśyapadāyādāḥ kīrtitāḥ sthāṇujamgamāḥ, |
teṣāṃ putrāś ca pautrāś ca śataśo 'tha sahasraśaḥ. || 109 ||
eṣa manvantare vipraḥ sargaḥ svārociṣe smṛtaḥ. |
vaivasvate tu mahati vāruṇe vitate kratau || 110 ||
juhvānasya brahmaṇo vai prajāsarga ihocyate. |
pūrvaṃ yatra tu brahmarṣīn utpannān sapta mānasān || 111 ||
putratve kalpayāmāsa svayam eva pitāmahaḥ, |
tato virodhe devānāṃ dānavānāṃ ca bho dvijaḥ. || 112 ||
ditir vinaṣṭaputrā vai toṣayāmāsa kaśyapaṃ. |
tāṃ kaśyapaḥ prasannātmā, samyag ārādhitas tayā, || 113 ||
vareṇa chandayāmāsa, sā ca vavre varaṃ tadā |
putram indravadhārthāya samartham amitaujasaṃ. || 114 ||
sa ca tasyai varaṃ prādāt prārthitaḥ sumahātapāḥ.
dattvā ca varam avyagro mārīcaḥ tām abhāṣata: || 115 ||
indraṃ putro nihantā te, garbhaṃ vai śaradāṃ śataṃ |
yadi dhārayase, śaucatatparā vratam āsthitā. || 116 ||
tathety abhihito bhartā tayā devyā mahātapāḥ. |
dhārayāmāsa garbhaṃ tu śuciḥ sā varavarṇinī. || 117 ||
tato 'bhyupāgamad dityāṃ | tatas tv ādhāya so dityāṃ
garbhaṃ ādhāya kaśyapaḥ | garbhaṃ tāṃ saṃśitavrataḥ |
rocay'an vai gaṇaśreṣṭhaṃ
devānām amitaujasāṃ. || 118 ||
tejaḥ samḥṛtya durdharṣaṃ
avadhyam amarair api |
jagāma parvatāyaiva
tapase saṃśitavrataḥ || 118 ||
tasyāś caivāntaraprepsur abhavat pākaśāsanaḥ, |
ūne varṣaśate cāsyā dadarśāntaram acyutaḥ. || 119 ||
akṛtvā pādayoḥ śaucaṃ ditiḥ śayanam āviśat |
nidrāṃ cāhārayāmāsa; tasyāḥ kukṣīṃ praviśya saḥ || 120 ||
vajrapāṇis tato garbhaṃ saptadhā taṃ nyakṛntata |
sa pāṭyamāno garbho 'tha vajreṇa praruroda ha, || 121 ||
mā rodīr iti taṃ śakraḥ punaḥ punar athābravīt. |
so 'bhavat saptadhā garbhas, tam indro ruṣitaḥ punaḥ || 122 ||
ekaikaṃ saptadhā cakre vajreṇaivārikarṣaṇaḥ. |
maruto nāma te devā babhūvuḥ sumahābalāḥ. || 123 ||
yathoktaṃ vai maghavatā, tathaiva maruto 'bhavan, |
devāś caikonapañcāsat saḥāyā vajrapāṇinaḥ. || 124 ||
teṣām evaṃ pravṛddhānāṃ |
bhūtānāṃ dvijasattamāḥ |
rocayan vai gaṇaśreṣṭhaṃ
devānām amitaujasāṃ, || 124 ||
nikāyeṣu nikāyeṣu
hariḥ prādāt prajāpatiḥ |
kramaśas tāni rājyāni pṛthupūrvāṇi bho dvijāḥ. || 125 ||
sa hariḥ puruṣo vīraḥ kṛṣṇo jiṣṇuḥ prajāpatiḥ |
parjanyaṣ tapano vyaktas tasya sarvam idaṃ jagat. || 126 ||
bhūtasargam imaṃ samyag
jānato dvijasattamāḥ |
marutāṃ ca śubhaṃ janma
śṛṇvataḥ paṭhato 'pi ca |
nāvṛttibhayam astīha,
paralokabhayaṃ kutaḥ. || 127 ||
teṣām eva pravṛddhānāṃ
hariḥ prādāt prajāpatiḥ |
bhūtasargam imaṃ samyak
kathitaṃ śṛṇu cāgrataḥ || 127 ||

## 2. Abschnitt. Vaṃśa | 3. Kapitel  (A. 19.22—29a; Bd. II. 8.1—26a; Br. 4.1—18; H. 257—282; Śidh. 54.95—5512; Vā. 70.1—21; Vi. I. 22.1—13a).

| evaṃ prajāsu sṛṣṭāsu kaśyapena mahātmanā |
| pratiṣṭhitāsu sarvāsu sthāvarāsu carāsu ca || 1 ||
abhiṣicyādhirājye tu
pṛṭhuṃ vainyaṃ pitāmahaḥ |
tataḥ krameṇa rājyāṇi vyādeṣṭum upacakrame. || 1 ||
dvijānāṃ vīrudhāṃ caiva nakṣatragrahayos tathā |
yajñānāṃ tapasāṃ caiva somaṃ rājye 'bhyāṣecayat. || 2 ||
apāṃ tu varuṇaṃ rājye rājñāṃ vaiśravaṇaṃ prabhuṃ, |
bṛhaspatiṃ tu viśveṣāṃ dadāv aṅgirasāṃ patiṃ. || 3 ||
bhṛgūṇām adhipaṃ caiva kāvyaṃ rājye 'bhyāṣecayat, |
ādityānāṃ tathā viṣṇuṃ, vasūnām atha pāvakaṃ, || 4 ||
prajāpatīnāṃ dakṣaṃ tu, marutām atha vāsavaṃ, |
daityānāṃ dānavānāṃ ca
prahlādam amitaujasaṃ. || 5 ||
nārāyaṇaṃ tu sādhyānāṃ rudrāṇāṃ vṛṣabhadhvajaṃ |
vipracittiṃ ca rājānaṃ dānavānām athādiśat. || 6 ||
vaivasvataṃ pitṛnāṃ ca yamaṃ rājye 'bhyāṣecayat |
yākṣāṇāṃ rākṣasāṇāṃ ca pārthivānāṃ tathaiva ca || 7 ||
sarvabhūtapiśācāṇāṃ girīśaṃ śūlapāṇinaṃ, |
śailānāṃ himavantaṃ ca, nadīnām atha sāgaraṃ, || 8 ||
gandhānāṃ marutāṃ caiva, bhutānām aśarīrīṇāṃ |
śabdākāśavatāṃ caiva vāyuṃ balavatāṃ varaṃ || 9 ||
sāgarāṇāṃ nadīnāṃ ca meghānāṃ varṣitasya ca. |
gandharvāṇām adhipatiṃ cakre citrarathaṃ prabhuṃ, || 10 ||
nāgānāṃ vāsukiṃ cakre,
sarpāṇām atha takṣakaṃ, |
sarveṣāṃ daṃṣṭrīṇāṃ śeṣaṃ
rājānam abhyāṣecayat. || 11 ||
vāraṇānāṃ tu rājānaṃ
airāvatam athādiśat, |
uccaiḥśravasam aśvānāṃ,
garuḍaṃ caiva pakṣiṇāṃ. || 12 ||
uccaiḥśravasam aśvānāṃ
rājānaṃ cābhyaṣecayat, |
pakṣiṇām atha sarveṣāṃ
garuḍaṃ patatāṃ varaṃ. || 12 ||
mṛgāṇām atha śārdūlaṃ, govṛṣaṃ tu gavām api |
vanaspatīnāṃ rājānaṃ
plakṣam evābhyaṣecayat. || 13 ||
sarvāpsarogaṇḍanāṃ ca kāmadevaṃ tathā prabhuṃ, |
ṛtūnām atha māsānāṃ dīvasānāṃ tathaiva ca || 14 ||
pakṣāṇāṃ ca kṣapāṇāṃ ca | pakṣānāṃ ca vipakṣānāṃ
muhūrtatithiparvaṇāṃ | muhūrtānāṃ ca parvaṇāṃ |
kalākāṣṭhāpramāṇānām ṛtor ayanayos tathā |
gaṇitasyātha yogasya cakre saṃvatsaraṃ prabhuṃ. || 15 ||
evaṃ vibhajya rājyāni
krameṇaiva pitāmahaḥ |
diśāṃ pālān atha tataḥ
sthāpayāmāsa sarvatah: || 15 ||
pūrvasyāṃ diśi putraṃ tu
vairājasya prājapateh |
diśa pālāṃ sudhanvānaṃ
rājānaṃ so 'bhyaṣecayat. || 16 ||
dakṣiṇasyāṃ diśi tathā kardamasya prājāpateh |
putraṃ śaṅkhapadaṃ nāma rājānaṃ so 'bhyaṣecayat. || 17 ||
paścimasyāṃ diśi tathā rajasaḥ putram acyutaṃ |
ketumantaṃ mahātmānaṃ rājānaṃ so 'bhyaṣecayat. || 18 ||
tathā hiraṇyaromāṇaṃ parjanyasya prājāpateh |
udīcyāṃ diśi durdharṣaṃ rājānaṃ so 'bhyaṣecayat. || 19 ||
| manuṣyāṇām adhipatiṃ cakre vaivasvataṃ manuṃ. |
taṃ iyaṃ prthivī sarvā saptadvīpā sapattanā |
yathāpradeśam adyāpi dharmeṇa pratipālyate. || 20 ||
| svāyambhuve 'ntare pūrvaṃ brahmaṇā te 'bhiṣecitāḥ, |
nṛpā hy ete 'bhiṣicyante, manavo ye bhavanti vai || 20 ||
manvantaresv atītesu gatā hy etesu pārthivāḥ. |
evam anye 'bhiṣicyante prāpte manvantare punaḥ. |
atītānāgatāḥ sarve smṛtā manvantareśvarāḥ. || 202 ||
rājasūyābhiṣiktas tu pṛthur ebhir narādhipaiḥ |
vedadṛṣṭena vidhinā rājarājye narādhīpaḥ. || 21 ||
tato manvantare 'tīte cākṣuṣe 'mitatejasi |
vaivasvatāya manave pṛthivyāṃ rājyam ādiśat. || 211 ||
tasya vistaram ākhyāsye manor vaivasvatasya ha |
bhavatāṃ cānukūlyāya, yadi śrotum ihecchatha; |
mahad hy etad adhiṣṭhānaṃ purāṇe pariniṣṭhitaṃ. || 212 ||
[dhanyaṃ yaśasyam āyuṣyaṃ svargavāsakaraṃ śubham.]

## 2. Abschnitt. Vaṃśa | 4. Kapitel  (Bd. I. 36.117—37.22a; Br. 4.19—122; H. 283—405; Śidh. 56.1—57.26;Vā. 62.99—63.21.)

rṣaya ūcuḥ:
vistareṇa pṛṭhor janma kīrtayasva mahāmate, |
yathā mahātmanā tena dugdhā pūrvaṃ vasuṃḍharā, || 1 ||
yathā ca pitṛbhir dugdhā,
yathā devair, mahārṣibhiḥ, |
yathā daityaiś ca nāgaiś ca,
yathā yakṣair, yathā drumaiḥ, || 2 ||
yathā śailaiḥ piśācaiś ca
gandharvaiś ca dvijottamaiḥ |
rākṣasaiś ca mahāsattvair,
yathā dugdhā vasuṃḍharā. || 3 ||
teṣāṃ pātraviśeṣāṃś ca
vaktum arhasi suvrata
vatsān kśīraviśeṣāṃś ca
dogdhāraṃ cānupūrvaśaḥ, || 4 ||
yasmāc ca kāraṇāt pāṇir venasya mathitaḥ purā |
kruddhair maharṣibhiḥ pūrvaiḥ, kāraṇaṃ tac ca kīrtaya || 5 ||
sūta (lomaharṣaṇa) uvāca:
kīrtayiṣyāmi vo vipraḥ pṛṭhor vainyasya saṃbhavaṃ, |
ekāgrāḥ prayatāś caiva śuśrūṣadhvaṃ dvijottamāḥ. || 6 ||
nāśuceḥ kṣudramanaso | nāśucer nāpi pāpāya
nāśiṣyāyāvratāya vā | nāśiṣyāyāhitāya ca |
kīrtayeyam idaṃ viprāḥ | varṇayeyam idaṃ brahma
kṛtaghnāyāhitāya vā. || 7 || nāvratāya kathaṃcana. || 7 ||
svargyaṃ yaśasyam āyuṣyaṃ dhanyaṃ vedaiś ca saṃmitaṃ |
rahasyam ṛṣibhiḥ proktaṃ śṛṇudhvaṃ vai yathātathaṃ! || 8 ||
yaṣ cemaṃ kathayen nityaṃ pṛthor vainyasya saṃbhavaṃ |
brāhmaṇebhyo namaskṛtya, na sa śocet kṛtākṛtaṃ. || 9 ||
āśīd dharmasya goptā vai | goptā dharmasya rājāsau
pūrvam atrisamaḥ prabhuḥ | babhūv-atrisamaḥ prabhuḥ |
atrivaṃśasamutpannas tv aṅgo nāma prajāpatiḥ. || 10 ||
tasya putro 'bhavad veno nātyarthaṃ dhārmikas tathā |
jāto mṛtyusutāyāṃ vai sunīthāyāṃ prajāpatiḥ. || 11 ||
sa mātāmahadoṣeṇa venāḥ kālātmajātmajaḥ |
svadharmaṃ pṛṣṭhataḥ kṛtvā kāmāl lobheṣv avartata. || 12 ||
maryādāṃ sthāpayāmāsa dharmāpetāṃ sa pārthivaḥ, |
vedadharmān atikramya so 'dharmanirato 'bhavat. || 13 ||
ayiṣyāmi vo viprāḥ; br. śṛṇudhvaṃ kīrtayiṣyāmi; h. hanta te
niḥsvādhyāyavaṣaṭkārāḥ prajās tasmin praśāsati |
prāvartan, na papuḥ somaṃ hutaṃ yajñeṣu devatāḥ. || 14 ||
na yaṣṭavyaṃ na hotavyam iti tasya prajāpateḥ |
āsīt pratijñā krūreyaṃ vināśe pratyupasthite. || 15 ||
aham ijyaś ca pūjyaś ca yajñe devaavijātibhiḥ, |
mayi yajñā vidhātavyā, mayi hotavyam ity api. || 16 ||
tam atikrāntamaryādam ādadānam asāmprataṃ |
ūcur maharṣayah sarve marīcipramukhās tadā: || 17 ||
vayaṃ dikṣāṃ pravekṣyāmaḥ samvatsaragaṇān bahūn, |
adharmaṃ kuru mā vena, eṣa dharmaḥ sanātanaḥ. || 18 ||
nidhane 'treḥ prasūtas tvaṃ prajāpatir asamśayaṃ, |
prajāś ca pālayiṣye 'ham iti te samayah kṛtah. || 19 ||
tāms tathā bruvataḥ sarvān maharṣīn abravīt tadā |
venāḥ prahasya durbuddhir imam artham anarthavit: || 20 ||
sraṣṭā dharmasya kāś cānyah, śrotavyaṃ kasya vā mayā, |
śrutavīryatapaḥsatyair mayā vā kah samo bhuvi? || 21 ||
prabhavaṃ sarvabhūtānāṃ dharmāṇāṃ ca viśeṣataḥ |
saṃmūḍhā na vidur nūnaṃ |
bhavanto māṃ acetasah. || 22 ||
icchan daheyaṃ pṛthivīṃ, plā vayeyaṃ jalais tathā |
dyāṃ bhuvaṃ caiva rundhreyaṃ,
nātra kāryā vicāraṇā. ||
yadā na śakyate mohād
avalepāc ca pārthivaḥ |
anunetuṃ tadā venas, tataḥ kruddhā maharṣayaḥ. || 24 ||
nigṛhya taṃ mahātmāno visphurantaṃ mahābalaṃ |
tato 'sya savyam ūruṃ te
mamanthur jātamanyavaḥ. || 25 ||
tasmin nimathyamāne vai
rājña ūrau tu jajñivān |
brasvo 'timātraṃ puruṣaḥ kṛṣṇaś cāpi babhūva ha. || 26 ||
sa bhītaḥ prāñjalir bhūtvā tasthivān vyākulendriyah. |
tam atrir vihvalaṃ drṣṭvā niṣīdety abravīt tadā. || 27 ||
niṣādavamśakartāsau babhūvānantavikramah, |
dhīvarān asrjac cāpi venakalmaṣasambhavān. || 28 ||
ye cānye vindhyaniḷayās tukhārās tumburās tathā |
adharmarucayaś cāpi, viddhi tān venakalmaṣān. || 29 ||
tataḥ punar mahātmānaḥ pāṇiṃ venasya dakṣiṇaṃ |
araṇīm iva saṃrabdhā mamanthur jātamanyavah. || 30 ||
prṭhus tasmāt samutpannaḥ karāj jvalanasaṃṇibhaḥ |
| prṭhoḥ karatalād vāpi yasmāj jātaḥ, prṭhus tataḥ. |
dīpyamānaḥ svavapuṣā sākṣād agnir iva jvalan || 31 ||
| [sa dhanvī kavacī jātaḥ prṭhur eva mahāyaśāḥ | ]
ādyam ājagavaṃ nāma dhanur gṛḥya mahāravaṃ |
śarāṃś ca divyān rakṣārthaṃ kavacaṃ ca mahāprabhaṃ. || 32 ||
tasmin jāte 'tha bhūtāni saṃprahrṣṭāni sarvaśāḥ |
samāpetur mahārājāṃ, venāś ca tridivaṃ yayau. || 33 ||
samutpannena rājarṣiḥ satputreṇa mahātmanā |
trātaḥ sa puruṣavyāghraḥ puṃnāmno narakāt tadā. || 34 ||
taṃ samudrāś ca nadyaś ca ratnāny ādāya sarvaśaḥ |
toyāni cābhiṣekārthaṃ sarva evopatasthire. || 35 ||
pitāmahaś ca bhagavān devair āṅgirasaiḥ saha |
sthāvarāṇi ca bhūtāni jaṅgamāni ca sarvaśaḥ || 36 ||
samāgamya tadā vainyam abhyaṣiñcan narādhīpaṃ |
mahatā rājarājyena prajāpālaṃ mahādyutiṃ. || 37 ||
so'bhiṣikto mahātejā | so 'bhiṣikto mahārājo
vidhivad dharmakovidaiḥ | devair aṅgirasaḥ sutaiḥ |
ādhirājye tadā rājñāṃ pṛthur vainyaḥ pratāpavān. || 38 ||
pitṛāparañjitās tasya prajās tenānurañjitāḥ; |
anurāgāt tatas tasya | tato rājeti nāmāsyā
nāma rājety ajāyata || 39 || a'nurāgād ajāyata. || 39 ||
āpas tastambhire tasya samudram abhiyāsyataḥ, |
parvatāś ca dadur mārgaṃ, dhvajabhaṅgaś ca nābhavat. || 40 ||
akṛṣṭapacyā pṛthivī, sidhyanty annāni cintayā, |
sarvakāmadughā gāvaḥ, puṭake puṭake madhu. || 41 ||
etasminn eva kāle tu yajñe paitāmahe śubhe |
sūtaḥ sūtyāṃ samutpannaḥ sautye 'hani mahāmatiḥ. || 42 ||
tasminn eva mahāyajñe jajñe prājño 'tha māgadhaḥ. |
sāmageṣu ca gāyatsu srugbhāṇḍe vaiśvadevake |
sāmagāne samutpannas, tasmān māgadha ucyate. || 42 ||
aindreṇa haviṣā cāpi haviḥ prktaṃ bṛhaspateḥ |
juhāv-endrāya devena, tataḥ sūto vyajāyata. || 42 ||
pramādas tatra samjajñe prāyaścittaṃ ca karmasu |
śiṣyahavyena yat prktam abhibhūtaṃ guror haviḥ || 42 ||
adharottaracāreṇa jajñe tadvarṇavaikṛtaṃ. |
yac ca kṣatrāt samabhavad brāhmaṇyaṃ hīnayonitaḥ, || 42 ||
sūtaḥ pūrveṇa sādharmyāt tulyadharmaḥ prakīrtitaḥ; |
madhyamo hy eṣa sūtasya dharmāḥ kṣatropajīvanaṃ, || 42 ||
rathanāgāśvacaritaṃ jaghanyaṃ ca cikitsitaṃ. |
prthoḥ stavārthaṃ tau tatra samāhūtau maharṣibhiḥ. || 43 ||
tāv ūcur rṣayaḥ sarve: stūyatām eṣa pārthivaḥ, |
karmaitad anurūpaṃ vāṃ, pātraṃ cāyaṃ narādhipaḥ. || 44 ||
tāv ūcatus tadā sarvāṃs tān rṣīn sūtamāgadhau: |
āvāṃ devān rṣīṃś caiva prīṇayāvaḥ svakarmabhiḥ. || 45 ||
na cāsyā vidvo vai karma na tathālakṣaṇaṃ yaśaḥ, |
stotraṃ yenāsyā kuryāva rājñas tejasvino dvijāḥ. || 46 ||
rṣibhis tau niyuktau tu: bhaviṣyaiḥ stūyatām iti, |
dānadharmarato nityaṃ satyavāk saṃjitendriyaḥ || 461 ||
jñānaśilo vadānyaś ca) samgrāmeṣv aparājitaḥ |
yāni karmāṇi kṛtavān pṛthuḥ paścān) mahābalaḥ. || 47 ||
satyavāg dānaśilo 'yaṃ satyasamḍho nareśvaraḥ |
śrīmāñ jaitraḥ kṣamāśilo vikrānto dṛṣṭāśāsanaḥ || 471 ||
dharmajñāś ca kṛtajñāś ca dayāvān priyabhāṣaṇaḥ |
mānyamānayitā yajvā brahmaṇyaḥ satyasamḍgarah |
śamaḥ śāntaḥ ca nirato vyavahārasthito nṛpaḥ. || 472 ||
tataḥ prabhṛti vai loke)
staveṣu munisattamāḥ) |
āśīrvādāḥ prayujyante
sūtamāgadhabandibhiḥ). || 48 ||
tayoḥ) stavānte supṛītaḥ pṛthuḥ prādāt prajeśvaraḥ |
anūpadeśaṃ sūtāya magadham) māgadbhāya ca. || 49 ||
taṃ dṛṣṭvā paramaprītāḥ prajāḥ) prāhur) maharṣayaḥ): |
vṛttīnām) eṣa vo dātā
bhaviṣyati narādhīpaḥ). || 50 ||
tato vainyaṃ mahābhāgaṃ) prajāḥ samabbidudruvuh: |
tvam) no vṛttiṃ vidhatsveti maharṣivacanāt tadā). || 51 ||
so 'bhidrutaḥ prajābhis tu prajāhitacikiṣayā |
dhanur gṛhya pṛṣatkāṃś ca
pṛthivīm ārdayad balī || 52 ||
tato vainyabbayatrastā gaur bhūtvā prādravan mahī,
tāṃ pṛthur dhanur ādāya dravantīm anvadhāvata. || 53 ||
sā lokān brahmālokādīn gatvā vainyabhayāt tadā |
pradadarśāgrato vainyaṃ pragṛhitaśarāsanaṃ || 54 ||
jvaladbhir niśitair bāṇair dīptatejasam acyutaṃ |
mahāyogaṃ mahātmānaṃ durdharṣaṃ amarair api. || 55 ||
alabhantī tu sā trāṇaṃ vainyam evānvapadyata |
kṛtāñjalipuṭā bhūtvā pūjyā lokais tribhiḥ sadā || 56 ||
uvāca vainyam: nādharmaṃ strīvadhe paripaśyasi? |
kathaṃ dhārayitā cāsi prajā rājan mayā vinā? || 57 ||
mayi lokāḥ sthitā rājan, mayedaṃ dhāryate jagat, |
mad rte ca vinaśyeyuḥ prajāḥ pārthiva, viddhi tat! || 58 ||
na mām arhasi hantuṃ vai, śreyaś cet tvaṃ cikīrṣasi |
prajānāṃ, pṛthivīpāla, śṛṇu cedaṃ vaco mama! || 59 ||
upāyataḥ samārabdhāḥ sarve sidhyanty upakramāḥ; |
| upāyaṃ paśya, yena tvaṃ dhārayethāḥ prajā nṛpa. || 60 ||
hatvapi māṃ na śaktas tvaṃ prajānāṃ pālāne nṛpa; |
anubhūtā bhaviṣyāmi, yaccha kopaṃ mahādyute! || 61 ||
avadhyāś ca striyah prāhus tiryagyonigateṣv api |
sattveṣu, pṛthivīpāla, na dharmaṃ tyaktum arhasi. || 62 ||
evaṃ bahuvidhaṃ vākyaṃ śrutvā rājā mahāmanāh |
kopaṃ nigṛhya dharmātmā vasudhām idam abravīt: || 63 ||
ekasyarthāya yo hanyād ātmano vā parasya vā |
bahūn vā prāṇino 'thaikam, | ekaṃ prāṇaṃ bahūn vāpi,
bhavet tasyeha pātakaṃ, || 64 || karma tasyasti pātakaṃ. || 64 ||
sukham edhanti bahavo | yasmiṃś tu nihate bhadre
yasmiṃś tu nihate 'śubhe, | edhante bahavaḥ sukhaṃ |
tasmin hate nāsti śubhe pātakaṃ copapātakaṃ. || 65 ||
[ekasmin yatra nidhanaṃ prāpite duṣṭakārīṇi |
bahūnāṃ bhavati kṣemaṃ, tatra puṇyaprado vadhah. ||]
so 'haṃ prajānimittaṃ tvāṃ haniṣyāmi vasuṃdhare, |
yadi me vacanaṃ nādyā kariṣyasi jagaddhitaṃ. || 66 ||
tvāṃ nihatyadyā bāṇena macchāsanaparāṅmukhīṃ |
ātmānaṃ prathayitvāhaṃ prajā dhārayitā svayaṃ. || 67 ||
sā tvaṃ śāsanam āsthāya mama dharmabhṛtāṃ vare |
saṃjīvaya prajāḥ sarvāḥ, | saṃjīvaya prajā nityaṃ,
samarthā hy asi dhāraṇe. || 68 || śaktā hy asi na saṃśayaḥ. || 68 ||
duhitṛtvaṃ ca me gaccha, tata enam ahaṃ śaraṃ |
niyaccheyaṃ tvadvadhārtham udyantaṃ ghoradarśanaṃ. || 69 ||
| pratyuvāca tato vainyam evam uktā satī mahī: |
sarvam etad ahaṃ vīra vidhāsyāmi na saṃśayaḥ. || 70 ||
| [upāyataḥ samārabdhāḥ sarve sidhyanty upakramāḥ; |
upāyaṃ paśya, yena tvaṃ dhārayethāḥ prajā imāḥ. || ]
vatsaṃ tu mama taṃ paśya, kṣareyaṃ yena vatsalā, |
samāṃ ca kuru sarvatra māṃ tvaṃ dharmabhṛtāṃ vara, || 71 ||
yathā viṣyandamānaṃ me kṣīraṃ sarvatra bhāvayet. |
tata utsārayāmāsa śailāñ śatasahasraśaḥ || 72 ||
dhanuṣkoṭyā tadā vainyas, tena śailā vivardhitāḥ. |
| [pṛthur vainyas tadā cakre mahīṃ rājā samāṃ tataḥ. | ]
manvantareṣv atīteṣu viṣamāṣīd vasuṃdharā, || 73 ||
svabhāvenabhavaṃs tasyāḥ samāni viṣamāṇi ca. |
cākṣuṣasyāntare pūrvam̃ āṣīd etat purā kila. || 74 ||
na hi pūrvavisarge vai viṣame) prthivītale |
pravibhāgaḥ purāṇāṃ vā) grāmāṇāṃ vābhavat tadā). || 75 ||
na sasyāni na gorakṣā) na krṣir na vaṇikpathaḥ |
| naiva satyāṇṛtaṃ cāsīn) na lobho na ca matsaraḥ. || 76 ||
vaivasvate 'ntare tasmin) sāṃprataṃ) samupasthite) |
vainyāt prabhṛti loke 'smin) sarvasyaitasya saṃbhavaḥ. || 77 ||
yatra yatra samaṃ tv asyā)
bhūmer āsīt tadā) dvijā), |
tatra tatra prajāḥ sarvā
nivāsaṃ samarocayan. || 78 ||
āhāraḥ) phalamūlani) prajāṇāṃ
kṛcchreṇa mahatā yukta), ity evam anuśūrama). || 79 ||
sa) kalpayitvā vatsaṃ tu
manuṃ svāyaṃbhuvaṃ prabhuṃ
svapāṇau puruṣavyāghro)
dudoha prthivīṃ tataḥ). || 80 ||
sasyajātāni sarvāṇi
prthur) vainyaḥ pratāpavān, |
tenannena prajāḥ sarvā)
vartante 'dyapi sarvaśāḥ). || 81 ||
ṛṣibhiḥ śrūyate) capi) punar dugdhā vasumḍharā, |
vatsaḥ somo 'bhavat) teṣām) dogdhā capi bṛhaspatiḥ), || 82 ||
pātram āsit tu chandāṃṣi) gāyātryādīni sarvaśāḥ), |
kṣīram āsit tadā teṣāṃ tapo) brahma) ca śāśvataṃ. || 83 ||
punas tato) devagaṇaiḥ puraṃḍarapurogamaiḥ) |
kāñcanaṃ) pātram ādāya dugdheyaṃ śrūyate mahī). || 84 ||
vatsas tu maghavān āsīd, dogdhā tu) savitā prabhuḥ), |
kṣīram ūrjaskaraṃ caiva), vartante yena) devatāḥ). || 85 ||
pitṛbhiḥ śrūyate capi) punar dugdhā vasumḍharā) |
rājataṃ pātram ādāya svadhām) amitavikramaiḥ). || 86 ||
| [svadhāmṛtaṃ ca pitṛṇām āsīd dogdh-aryamā tathā, | ]
yamo vaivasvatas teṣām) āsīd) vatsaḥ pratāpavān), |
| antakaś cabhavad dogdhā kālo lokaprakālanaḥ). || 87 ||
nāgaiś ca śrūyate dugdhā vatsaṃ kṛtvā tu takṣakaṃ |
alābuṃ pātram ādāya viṣaṃ kṣīraṃ tadā mahī. || 88 ||
nāgānāṃ vai dvijaśreṣṭha sarpāṇāṃ caiva sarvaśaḥ || 89 ||
asuraiḥ śrūyate cāpi punar dugdhā vasuṃḍharā |
āyasaṃ pātram ādāya
māyāṃ śatrūnibarhiṇīṃ. || 91 ||
virocanas tu prāhrādir vatsas teṣām abhūt tadā, |
ṛtvig dvimūrdhā daityānāṃ,
madhur dogdhā mahābalaḥ. || 92 ||
tayaite māyayādyāpi
sarve māyāvino 'surāḥ |
vartayanty amitaprajñās,
tad eṣām amitaṃ balaṃ. || 93 ||
| yakṣaiś ca śrūyate rājan punar dugdhā vasuṃḍharā |
āmapātre punar dugdhā tv antarḍhānam iyaṃ mahī || 94 ||
tena kṣīreṇa yakṣāś ca
rākṣasāś cāmaropamāḥ |
vartayanti piśācāś ca
bhūtasaṅghās tathaiva ca. || 99 ||
padmapātre punar dugdhā gandharvaiḥ sāpsarogaṇaiḥ |
vatsaṃ citrarathaṃ kṛtvā śucīn gandhāms tathaiva ca. || 100 ||
teṣāṃ vasuruciś tv āsīd dogdhā putro muneḥ śuciḥ |
gandharvarājo 'tibalo mahātmā sūryasaṃnibhaḥ. || 101 ||
śailaiś ca śrūyate dugdhā punar devī vasuṃḍharā |
tatrauṣadhīr mūrtimatī ratnāni vividhāni ca. || 102 ||
vatsas tu himavān āsīd, dogdhā merur mahāgiriḥ, |
| pātraṃ tu śailam evāsīt, tena śailāḥ pratiṣṭhitāḥ. || 103 ||
| śrūyate vṛkṣavīrudbhiḥ punar dugdhā vasumḍharā |
pālāśaṃ pātram ādāya chinnadagdhaprarohaṇaṃ. || 104 ||
kāmadhuk puṣpitaḥ śālāḥ plakṣo vatso yaśasvināṃ, |
sarvakāmadughā dogdhrī | sarvakāmadughā dogdhrī
sarvasasyaprarohaṇī. || 105 || | pṛthivī bhūtabhāvinī. || 105 ||
seyaṃ dhātrī vidhātrī ca pāvanī ca vasumḍharā |
| dugdhā hitārthaṃ lokānāṃ pṛṭhuneti hi naḥ śrutaṃ. |
carācarasya sarvasya pratiṣṭhā yonir eva ca || 106 ||
āsīd iyaṃ samudrāntā medinīti pariśrutā. |
| vasu dhārayate yasmād, vasudhā tena cocyate. |
madhu-kaiṭabhayoḥ kṛtsnā medasā saṃpariplutā, || 107 ||
teneyaṃ medinī devī procyate brahmavādibhiḥ. |
tato' bhyupagamād rājñāḥ pṛṭhor vainyasya dhīmataḥ || 108 ||
duhitṛtvam anuprāptā devī pṛṭhvīti cocyate. |
pṛthunā pravibhaktā ca śodhitā ca vasumḍharā || 109 ||
sasyākaravatī sphītā
purapattanamālinī. || 110 ||
evamprabhāvo vainyaḥ sa rājāsīd rājasattamaḥ |
namasyaś caiva pūjyaś ca bhūtagrāmair na saṃśayaḥ. || 111 ||
brāhmaṇaiś ca mahābhāgair vedavedāṅgapāragaiḥ |
pṛthur eva namaskāryo brahmayoniḥ sanātanaḥ. || 112 ||
pārthivaiś ca mahābhāgaiḥ pārthivatvam abhīpsubhiḥ |
ādirājo namaskāryaḥ pṛthur vainyaḥ pratāpavān, || 113 ||
yodhair api ca vikrāntaiḥ prāptukāmair jayaṃ yudhi. |
ādikartā raṇānāṃ ca | ādikartā raṇānāṃ vai
yodhānāṃ prathamo nṛpaḥ. 114 | namasyaḥ prthur eva hi. || 114 ||
yo hi yoddhā raṇaṃ yāti kīrtayitvā pṛthuṃ nṛpaṃ, |
sa ghorarūpān saṃgrāmān kṣemī tarati kīrtimān. || 115 ||
vaiśyair api ca vittāḍhyair vaiśyavṛttividhāyibhiḥ |
pṛthur eva namaskāryo vṛttidātā mahāyaśāḥ. || 116 ||
tathaiva śūdraiḥ śucibhis trivarṇaparicāribhiḥ |
pṛthur eva namaskāryaḥ śreyāḥ param abhipsubhiḥ. || 116 ||
ete vatsaviśeṣāś ca dogdhāraḥ kṣīram eva ca |
pātrāṇi ca mayoktāni,
kiṃ bhūyo varṇayāmi vaḥ? || 117

## 3. Abschnitt. Manvantara  (Bd. III. 1.3—116; Br. 5.1—64; H. 406—544; Śidh. 58. 2b—82; Vā. 100.3—118;cfr. Mr. 94.)

ṛṣaya ūcuḥ:
manvantarāṇi sarvāṇi vistareṇa mahāmāte |
teṣāṃ pūrvaviṣṛṣṭiṃ ca lomaharṣaṇa kīrtaya! || 1 ||
yāvanto manavaś caiva yāvantaṃ kālam eva ca |
manvantarāṇi bho sūta śrotum icchāma tattvataḥ! || 2 ||
sūta (lomaharṣaṇa) uvāca:
na śakyo vistaro viprā vaktuṃ varṣaśatair api |
manvantarāṇāṃ sarveṣāṃ saṃkṣepāc chṛṇuta dvijāḥ! || 3 ||
svāyaṃbhuvo manuḥ pūrvaṃ manuḥ svārociṣas tathā |
auttamas tāmasaś caiva raivataś cākṣuṣas tathā || 4 ||
vaivasvataś ca bho viprāḥ sāṃprataṃ manur ucyate, |
sāvarṇiś ca manus tadvad bhautyo raucyas tathaiva ca || 5 ||
tathaiva merusāvarṇāś catvāro manavaḥ smṛtāḥ. |
atītā vartamānāś ca tathaivanāgatāś ca ye, || 6 ||
kīrtitā manavas tubhyaṃ mayaivaite yathā śrutāḥ. |
ṛṣīṃs teṣāṃ pravakṣyāmi putrān devagaṇāṃs tathā: || 7 ||
marīcir atrir bhagavān aṅgirāḥ pulahāḥ kratuḥ |
pulastyaś ca vasiṣṭhaś ca saptaite brahmaṇāḥ sutāḥ. || 8 ||
uttarasyāṃ diśi tathā dvijāḥ saptarṣayas tathā. |
yāmā nāma tathā devā āsan svāyambhuve 'ntare. || 9 ||
āgnīdhraś-c-āgnibāhuś ca medhā. medhātithir vasuḥ |
jyotiṣmān dyutimān havyaḥ savanāḥ putra eva ca, || 10 ||
manoḥ svāyambhuvasyaite daśa putrā mahaujasaḥ. |
etad vai prathamaṃ viprā manvantaraṃ udāhṛtaṃ. || 11 ||
aurvo vasiṣṭhaputraś ca stambaḥ kāśyapa eva ca |
prāṇo bṛhaspatiś caiva | bhārgavaś ca tathā prāṇa
datto niścyavanas tathā, || 12 || | rṣabho 'ṅgirasas tathā |
| paulastyaś caiva dattātrir |
| ātreyo niścalas tathā |
| paulaho 'th-ārvarīvāṃś ca,
| ete saptarṣayas tathā. || 12 ||
ete maharṣayas tatra vāyuproktā mahāvratāḥ |
devāś ca tuṣitā nāma smṛtāḥ svārociṣe 'ntare. || 13 ||
havidhraḥ sukṛtir, jyotir āpo mūrtir ayasmayaḥ |
prathītaś ca nabhasyaś ca nabha ūrjas tathaiva ca || 14 ||
svārociṣasya putrās te manor viprā mahātmanāḥ |
kīrtitāḥ pṛthivīpālā mahāvīryaparākramāḥ. || 15 ||
dvitīyam etat kathitaṃ viprā manvantaraṃ mayā. |
idaṃ tṛtīyaṃ vakṣyāmi, tad budhyadhvaṃ dvijottamāḥ! || 16
vasiṣṭhaputrāḥ saptāsan vāsiṣṭhā iti viśrutāḥ |
hiraṇyagarbhasya sutā ūrjā nāma sutejasaḥ. || 17 ||
ṛṣayo 'tra mayā proktāḥ, kīrtyamānān nibodhata |
auttameyān muniśreṣṭhā daśa putrān manoramān: || 18 ||
iṣa ūrjas tanūrjaś ca madhur mādhava eva ca |
śuciḥ śukraḥ sahaś caiva nabhasyo nabha eva ca. || 19 ||
bhānavas tatra devāś ca; manvantaram udāḥṛtaṃ. |
manvantaraṃ caturthaṃ tu kathayiṣyāmi sāṃprataṃ. || 20 ||
kāvyaḥ pṛṭhus tathaiv-agnir janyur dhātā dvijottamāḥ |
kapīvān akapīvāṃś ca tatra saptarṣayo dvijāḥ, || 21 ||
purāṇe kīrtitā viprāḥ putrāḥ pautrāś ca bho dvijāḥ. |
satyā devagaṇāś caiva tāmasasyāntare manoḥ. || 22 ||
[putrāṃś caiva pravakṣyāmi tāmasasya manor nṛpa: |]
dyutis tapasyaḥ sutapās tapomūlas tapośanāḥ |
taporatir akalmāṣas tanvī dhanvī paraṃtapāḥ. || 23 ||
tāmasasya manor ete daśa putrā mahābalāḥ |
vāyuproktā muniśreṣṭhāś, caturthaṃ caitad antaraṃ. || 24 ||
vedabāhur yadudhraś ca munir vedaśirās tathā |
hiraṇyaromā parjanya ūrdhvabāhuś ca somapaḥ || 25 ||
satyanetras tath-atreyā ete saptarṣayo 'pare |
devāś c-abhūtarajasas tathā prakṛtayo 'pare || 26 ||
pāriplavaś ca raibhyaś ca manor antaram ucyate. |
atha putrān imāṃs tasya budhyadhvaṃ gadato mama: || 27 ||
dhṛtimān avyayo yuktas tattvadarśī nirutsukah |
araṇyaś ca prakāśaś ca nirmohah satyavān kṛtī, || 28 ||
raivatasya manoh putrāh; pañcamaṃ caitad antaraṃ. |
ṣaṣṭhaṃ tu saṃpravakṣyāmi, tad budhyadhvaṃ dvijottamāh: || 29 ||
bhṛgur nabho vivasvāṃś ca sudhāmā virajās tathā |
atināmā sahiṣṇuś ca saptaite tu maharṣayah. || 30 ||
cākṣuṣasyantare viprā manor devās tv ime smṛtāh: |
ādyah prabhūtā rbhavah pṛthukāś ca divaukasah || 31 ||
lekhāś ca nāmato viprāh pañca devagaṇāh smṛtāh. |
ṛṣer aṅgirasah putrā mahātmāno mahaujasah || 32 ||
nāḍvaleyā muniśreṣṭhā daśa putrās tu viśrutāh |
ūruprabhṛtayo viprāś cākṣuṣasyantare manoh. || 33 ||
ṣaṣṭhaṃ manvantaraṃ proktaṃ; saptamaṃ tu nibodhata: |
atrir vasiṣṭho bhagavān kaśyapaś ca mahān ṛṣih || 34 ||
gautamo 'tha bharadvājo viśvāmitras tathaiva ca |
tathaiva putro bhagavān ṛcīkasya mahātmānah || 35 ||
saptamo jamadagniś ca ṛṣayaḥ sāmprataṃ divi. |
sādhyā rudrāś ca viśve ca vasavo marutas tathā || 36 ||
ādityāś c-āśvinau caiva devau vaivasvatau smṛtau |
manor vaivasvatasyaite vartante sāmprate 'ntare. || 37 ||
ikṣvākupramukhāś caiva (daśa putrā) mahātmanaḥ |
eteṣāṃ kīrtitānāṃ tu maharṣīṇāṃ mahaujasāṃ. || 38 ||
teṣāṃ putrāś ca pautrāś ca dikṣu sarvāsu bho dvijāḥ |
manvantaṛeṣu sarveṣu prāg āsan sapta saptakāḥ. || 39 ||
sthitā dharmavyavasthārthaṃ lokasaṃrakṣaṇāya ca |
manvantare vyatīkrānte catvāraḥ saptakā gaṇāḥ. || 40 ||
kṛtvā karma divaṃ yānti brahmalokam anāmayaṃ. |
tato 'nye tapasā yuktāḥ sthānaṃ tat pūrayanty uta |
atītā vartamānāś ca krameṇaitena bho dvijāḥ. || 41 ||
ṛṣaya ūcuḥ:
manvantarāṇi sarvāṇi pūrvāṇy evāparaiḥ saha |
saptarṣīṇām athaiteṣāṃ sāmpratasyāntare manoḥ || 41 ||
vistarāvayavaṃ caiva nisargasya mahātmanaḥ |
vistareṇānupūrvyā ca sarvam eva bravihi naḥ. || 41 ||
sūta uvāca:
bhavatāṃ kathayiṣyāmi sarvam etad yathātathaṃ. |
pādaṃ tv imaṃ sasaṃhāraṃ caturthaṃ munisattamāḥ || 41 ||
manor vaivasvatasyemaṃ sāmpratasya mahātmanaḥ |
vistareṇānupūrvyā ca nisargaṃ śṛṇuta dvijāḥ! || 41 ||
manvantarāṇāṃ saṃkṣepaṃ bhaviṣyaiḥ saha saptabhiḥ |
pralayaṃ caiva lokānāṃ bruvato me nibodhata! || 41 ||
etāny uktāni vai samyak sapta saptasu vai mayā, |
manvantarāṇi saṃkṣepāc chṛṇutānāgatāni me! || 42 ||
sāvarṇā manavo bhavyāḥ pañca, tāṃś ca nibodhata: |
eko vaivasvatas teṣāṃ, catvāras tu prajāpateḥ || 43 ||
parameṣṭhisutā vipra merusāvarṇatāṃ gatāḥ, |
dakṣasyaite hi dauhitrāḥ priyāyās tanayā nṛpāḥ, || 44 ||
mahatā tapasā yuktā merupṛṣṭhe mahaujasah. |
ruceḥ prajāpateḥ putro raucyo nāma manuḥ smṛtah, |
bhūtyāṃ cotpādito devyāṃ bhautyo nāma ruceḥ sutah. || 45 ||
sāvarṇasya pravakṣyāmi manor vaivasvatasya ha |
bhaviṣyasya bhaviṣyaṃ tu samāsāt, tan nibodhata! || 45 ||
anāgatāś ca saptaite smṛtā divi maharṣayah |
manor antaram āsādyā
sāvarṇasyeha bho dvijāḥ: || 46 ||
rāmo vyāsas tath-atreyo
dīptimān iti viśrutah |
kauśiko galavaś caiva
jāmadagnyaś ca bhārgavaḥ || 46 ||
dvaipāyano vasiṣṭhaś ca
kṛpah śāradvatas tathā |
atreyo dīptimāṃś caiva
rṣyaśṛṅgas tu kāśyapaḥ |
bhāradvājas tathā drauṇir aśvatthāmā mahādyutiḥ || 47 ||
gautamasyatmajaś caiva
śaradvān nāma gautamaḥ |
kauśiko gālavaś caiva
ruruh kāśyapa eva ca, |
ete sapta mahātmāno bhaviṣyā munisattamāḥ. || 48 ||
brahmaṇaḥ sadṛśāś caite
dhanyāḥ saptarṣayaḥ smṛtāḥ |
abhijātyatha tapasā
mantravyākaraṇais tathā. || 481 ||
brahmalokapratiṣṭhās tu
smṛtā brahmarṣayo 'malāḥ |
bhūtabhavyabhava[j]jñānaṃ
buddhvā caiva ca ye svayaṃ, || 482 ||
tapasā tu prasiddhā ye
saṃgatāḥ pravicintakāḥ |
mantravyākaraṇādyaiś ca
aiśvaryāt sarvaśaś ca ye, || 483 ||
etān bhāryānvito jñātvā
naiṣṭhikāni ca nāma ca |
saptaite saptabhiś caiva
guṇaiḥ saptarṣayaḥ smṛtāḥ, || 484 ||
dīrghāyuṣo mantrakṛta
iśvarā dīrghacakṣuṣaḥ |
buddhyā pratyakṣadharmāṇo
gotraprāvartakās tathā. || 485 ||
kṛtādiṣu yugākhyeṣu
sarveṣv eva punaḥ punaḥ |
sutapāś c-amitābhāś ca
mukhyāś caiva gaṇās trayāḥ, |
teṣāṃ gaṇās tu devānāṃ
ekaiko viṃśakaḥ smṛtāḥ. || 481 ||
nāmatas tu pravakṣyāmi
nibodhadhvaṃ samāhitāḥ: |
rtus tapaś ca śukraś ca
dyutir jyotiḥ prabhākaraḥ || 482 ||
prabhāso bhāsakṛd dharmaś
tejo raśmiḥ kratur virāṭ |
arciṣmān dyotano bhānur
yaśaḥ kīrtir budho dhṛtiḥ, |
viṃśatiḥ sutapā hy ete
nāmabhiḥ parikīrtitāḥ. || 483 ||
prabhur vibhur vibhāsaś ca
jetā hant-arihā rtuḥ |
sumatiḥ pramatiṛ dīptiḥ
samākhyāṭo maho mahān || 484 ||
deho munir nayo jyeṣṭhaḥ
samāḥ satyaś ca viśrutāḥ, |
ity ete hy amitābhās tu
viṃśatiḥ parikīrtitāḥ. || 485 ||
pravartayanti te varṇān
āśramāṃś caiva sarvaśaḥ || 486 ||
saptarṣayo mahābhāgāḥ
satyadharmaparāyaṇāḥ. |
teṣāṃ caivānvayotpannā
jāyantiḥa punaḥ punaḥ || 487 ||
mantrabrāhmaṇakartāro
dharme praśithile tathā. |
yasmāc ca varadāḥ sapta
parebhya eva yācitāḥ, || 488 ||
tasmān na kālo na vayāḥ
pramāṇam ṛṣibhāvane. |
ete(!) saptarṣikoddeśo
vyākhyātas te mayā nṛpa. || 489 ||
mārīcasyaiva te putrāḥ kaśyapasya mahātmanaḥ || 49 ||
| sāṃpratasya bhaviṣyanti sāvarṇasyāntare manoḥ, |
| teṣām indro bhaviṣyas tu balir vairocanaḥ purā. || 491 ||
vīravāṃś c-āvarīyāṃś ca sumanto dhṛtimān vasuḥ |
cariṣṇur āryo dhṛṣṇuś ca vājaḥ sumatir eva ca |
sāvarṇasya manoḥ putrā bhaviṣyā munisattamāḥ. || 50 ||
| nava cānyeṣu vakṣyāmi sāvarṇeṣv antaṛeṣu vai. |
| sāvarṇamanavāś cānye bhaviṣyā brahmaṇaḥ sutāḥ || 501 ||
merusāvarṇitas te vai catvāro divyadṛṣṭayah. |
dakṣasya te hi dauhitrāḥ kriyāyā duhituḥ sutāḥ, || 502 ||
mahatā tapasā yuktā merupṛṣṭhe mahaujasaḥ |
brahmādibhis te janitā dakṣeṇaiva ca dhimatā. || 503 ||
maharlokaṃ gatāvṛttā bhaviṣyā merum āśritāḥ |
mahānubhāvās te pūrvaṃ jajñire cākṣuṣe 'ntare: || 504 ||
ṛṣaya ūcuḥ:
dakṣeṇa janitāḥ putrāḥ kanyāyām ātmanaḥ kathaṃ |
bhavena brahmaṇā caiva dharmeṇa ca mahātmanaḥ(!)? || 505 ||
sūta uvāca:
ato bhaviṣyān vakṣyāmi sāvarṇamanavas tu ye, |
teṣāṃ janma prabhāvaṃ ca namas kṛtvā pracetase. || 506 ||
vaivasvate hy upaspṛṣṭe kimcicchiṣṭe ca cākṣuṣe |
jajñire manavas te hi bhaviṣyānāgatāntare || 507 ||
prācetāsasya dakṣasya dauhitrā manavas tu ye |
sāvarṇā nāmataḥ pañca catvāraḥ paramarṣijāḥ. || 508 ||
saṃjñā-putras tu sāvarṇa eko vaivasvatas tathā |
jyeṣṭhaḥ saṃjñā-suto nāma manur vaivasvataḥ prabhuḥ, || 509 ||
vaivasvate 'ntare prāpte samutpattis tayoḥ śubhā. |
