pūro) prīto 'smi, bhadraṃ te, grhāṇa tvam) svayauvanaṃ |
rājyaṃ ca tvam) grhaṇedam). tvaṃ hi me priyakṛt sutaḥ. || 68 ||
pratipede jarāṃ rājā yayātir nahuṣātmajaḥ), |
yauvanaṃ pratipede ca) pūruḥ) svaṃ punar ātmanaḥ. || 69 ||
abhiṣektukāmaṃ ca nṛpaṃ) pūrūṃ) putraṃ kanīyasaṃ |
brāhmaṇapramukhā varṇā idaṃ vacanam abruvan: || 70 ||
kathaṃ śukrasya naptāram) devayānyāḥ sutaṃ prabho |
jyeṣṭham) yadum atikramya rājyaṃ) pūroḥ) pradāsyasi)? || 71 ||
yadur) jyeṣṭhas) tava suto, jātas tam anu turvasuḥ), |
śarmiṣṭhāyāḥ suto druhyus), tato 'nuḥ) pūrur) eva ca. || 72 ||
kathaṃ jyeṣṭhān atikramya kanīyān rājyam arhati, |
etat saṃbodhayāmas tvāṃ, dharmaṃ samanupālaya! || 73 ||
yayātir uvāca:
brāhmaṇapramukhā varṇāḥ sarve śṛṇvantu me vacaḥ, |
jyeṣṭhaṃ prati yathā rājyaṃ na deyaṃ me kathamcana. || 74 ||
mātāpitror vacanakṛd dhitaḥ putraḥ praśasyate, |
mama jyeṣṭhena yadunā niyogo nānupālitaḥ. || 75 ||
pratikūlaḥ pitur yaś ca, na sa putraḥ satāṃ mataḥ, |
sa putraḥ, putravad yaś ca vartate pitṛmāṭṛṣu. || 76 ||
yadunāham avajñātas tathā turvasunāpi ca |
druhyuṇā c-ānunā caiva mayy avajñā kṛtā bhṛśaṃ. || 77 ||
pūruṇā tu kṛtaṃ vākyaṃ mānitaṃ ca viśeṣataḥ, |
kanīyān mama dāyādo, jarā yena dhṛtā mama. || 78 ||
sarve kāmā mama kṛtāḥ pūruṇā putrarūpiṇā, |
śukreṇa ca varo dattaḥ kāvyen-ṓśanasā svayam: || 79 ||
putro yas tvānuvarteta, sa rājā prthivīpate! |
bhavato 'numato 'py evaṃ pūrū rājye 'bhiṣicyatāṃ. || 80 ||
prajā (prakṛtaya) ūcuḥ:
yaḥ putro guṇasaṃpanno mātāpitror hitaḥ sadā, |
sarvam arhati kalyāṇaṃ kanīyān api sa prabhuḥ. || 81 ||
arhaḥ pūruṛ idaṃ rājyaṃ, yaḥ priyaḥ priyakṛt tava, |
varadānena śukrasya na śakyaṃ vaktum uttaraṃ. || 82 ||
paurajānapadais tuṣṭair ity ukto nāhuṣas tadā. |
abhiṣicya tataḥ pūruṃ rājye sve sutam ātmanaḥ || 83 ||
diśi dakṣiṇapūrvasyāṃ turvasuṃ tu nyaveśayat, |
diśi pūrvottarasyāṃ tu
yaduṃ jyeṣṭhaṃ nyayojayat,
[madhye pūruṃ ca rājānaṃ
abhyāṣiñcat sa nāhuṣaḥ.] || 84 ||
pratīcyām uttarasyāṃ ca druhyuṃ c-ānuṃ ca tāv ubhau. |
saptadvīpāṃ yayātis tu jitvā pṛthvīṃ sasāgarāṃ |
vyabhajat pañcadhā rājā putrebhyo nāhuṣas tadā. || 85 ||
tair iyaṃ pṛthivī sarvā saptadvīpā sapattanā |
yathāpradeśaṃ dharmajñair dharmeṇa pratipālyate. || 86 ||
[prajās teṣāṃ purastāt tu
vakṣyāmi munisattamaḥ. | ]
dhanur nyasya pṛsatkāṃś ca pañcabhīḥ puruṣarṣabhaiḥ |
jarāvān abhavad rājā bhāram āveśya bandhuṣu. || 87 ||
| nikṣiptaśastraḥ pṛthivīṃ nirīkṣya pṛthivīpatīḥ |
| prītimān abhavad rajā yayātir aparājitaḥ. || 87 ||
atra gāthā mahārājñā purā gītā yayātīnā, |
yābhiḥ pratyāharet kāmān sarvaśo ''ṅgāni kūrmavat : || 88 ||
na jātu kāmāḥ kāmānām upabhogena śāmyati, |
haviṣā krṣṇavartmeva bhūya evābhivardhate. || 89 ||
yat pṛthivyāṃ vrīhiyavaṃ hiraṇyaṃ paśavaḥ striyaḥ, |
nālam ekasya tat sarvam, iti kṛtvā na muhyati. || 90 ||
yadā na kurute bhāvaṃ sarvabhūteṣu pāpakaṃ |
karmaṇā manasā vācā, brahma saṃpadyate tadā. || 91 ||
yadā parān na bibheti, yadā cāsmāu na bibhyati, |
yadā necchati, na dveṣṭi, brahma saṃpadyate tadā. || 92 ||
yā dustyajā durmatibhir, yā na jīryati jīryataḥ, |
yo 'sau prāṇāntiko rogas, tāṃ trṣṇāṃ tyajataḥ sukhaṃ. || 93 ||
jīryanti jīryataḥ keśā, dantā jīryanti jīryataḥ, |
dhanāśā jīvitāśā ca jīryato 'pi na jīryati. || 94 ||
yac ca kāmasukhaṃ loke, yac ca divyaṃ mahat sukhāṃ, |
trṣ'ṇākṣayasukhasyaitat kalāṃ nārhati ṣoḍaśīṃ. || 95 ||
[pūrṇaṃ varṣasahasraṃ me viṣayāsakṭacetasaḥ, |
tathāpy anudinaṃ trṣṇā mamaiteṣv abhijāyate. || 951 ||
tasmād enām ahaṃ tyaktvā brahmaṇy ādhāva mānasaṃ |
nirdvandvo nirmamo bhūtvā cariṣyāmi mṛgaiḥ saha.] || 952 ||
evam uktvā sa rājarṣiḥ sadārah prāviśad vanaṃ |
kālena mahatā cāpi
cacāra vipulaṃ tapah. || 96 ||
bhṛgutuṅge tapas tīrtvā
tapaso 'nte mahāyaśāḥ |
anaśnan deham utsrīya
sadārah svargam āptavān. || 97 ||
tasya vamśe munīśreṣṭhāḥ
pañca rājarṣiṣattamāḥ, |
yair vyāptā prthivī krṭsnā sūryasyeva gabhastibhiḥ. || 98 ||
dhanyaḥ prajāvān āyuṣmān kīrtimāmś ca bhaven narah |
yayāteś caritaṃ puṇyaṃ paṭhañ śrṇvan dvijottamāḥ. || 99 ||

## 4. Abschnitt. Vaṃśānucarita | 4. Kapitel

praseno dvāravatyāṃ tu nivasan yo mahāmaṇiṃ |
divyaṃ syamantakaṃ nāma sa sūryād upalabdhavān || 1 ||
tasya satrājitaḥ sūryaḥ sakhā prāṇasamo 'bhavat. |
sa kadācin niśāpāye rathena rathināṃ varaḥ || 1 ||
toyaṃ kūlād upaspraṣṭum upasthātuṃ yayau raviṃ. |
tasyopatiṣṭhataḥ sūryaṃ vivasvān agrataḥ sthitaḥ || 2 ||
aspaṣṭamūrtir bhagavāms tejomaṇḍalavān vibhuḥ. |
atha rājā vivasvantam uvāca sthitam agrataḥ: || 3 ||
yathaiva vyomni paśyāmi sadā tvāṃ jyotiṣāṃ pate |
tejomaṇḍalinaṃ devaṃ tathaiva purataḥ sthitaṃ, || 4 ||
ko viśeṣo 'sti me tvattaḥ sakhyenopagatasya vai? |
etac chrutvā sa bhagavān maṇiratnaṃ syamantakaṃ || 5 ||
svakaṇṭhād avamucyātha ekānte nyastavān vibhuḥ. |
tato vigrahavantaṃ taṃ dadarśa nṛpatis tadā. || 6 ||
prītimān atha taṃ drṣṭvā muhūrtaṃ kṛṭavān kathāṃ. |
tam abhiprasthitaṃ bhūyo vivasvantaṃ sa satrajit || 7 ||
lokān udbhāsayasy etān | provācāgnisavarṇaṃ tvāṃ,
yena tvaṃ satataṃ prabho, | yena lokaḥ prapaśyati, |
tad etan maṇiratnaṃ me bhagavan dātum arhasi! || 8 ||
tataḥ syamantakamaṇiṃ dattavāms tasya bhāskaraḥ. |
sa tam ābadhya nagarīṃ praviveśa mahīpatiḥ. || 9 ||
taṃ janāḥ paryadhāvanta: sūryo 'yaṃ gacchatīti ha! |
svāṃ purīṃ sa visiṣmāya rājā tv antaḥpuraṃ tathā. || 10 ||
taṃ prasenajite divyaṃ maṇiratnaṃ syamantakaṃ |
dadau bhrātre narapatiḥ premṇā satrājid uttamaṃ. || 11 ||
sa maṇiḥ syandate rukmaṃ | syamantako nāma maṇir
vṛṣṇy-andhakaniveśane, | yasmin rāṣṭre sthito bhavet, |
kālavarṣī ca parjanyo na ca vyādhibhayaṃ hy abhūt. || 12 ||
lipsāṃcakre prasenāṭ tu maṇiratne syamantake |
govindo, na ca taṃ lebhe, śakto 'pi na jahāra saḥ. || 13 ||
kadācin mṛgayāṃ yātaḥ prasenas tena bhūṣitaḥ |
syamantakakṛṭe siṃhād vadhaṃ prāpa vanecarāt. || 14 ||
atha siṃhaṃ pradhāvantaṃ
ṛkṣarājo mahābalaḥ |
nihatyā maṇiratnaṃ tad
ādāya bilam āviśat. || 15 ||
tato vṛṣṇy-andhakāḥ kṛṣṇaṃ
prasenavadhakāraṇāt |
prārthanāṃ tāṃ maṇer buddhvā
sarva eva śaśaṅkire. || 16 ||
sa śaṅkyamāno dharmātmā
akārī tasya karmaṇaḥ, |
āhariṣye maṇim iti
pratijñāya vanaṃ yayau. || 17 ||
yatra praseno mṛgayām acarat tatra capy atha |
prasenasya padaṃ gṛhya puruṣair āptakārībhī. || 18 ||
ṛkṣavantaṃ girivaraṃ vindhyaṃ ca girim uttamaṃ |
anveṣayan pariśrāntaḥ sa dadarśa mahāmanāḥ || 19 ||
sāśvaṃ hataṃ prasenaṃ tu, nāvindata ca taṃ maṇiṃ. |
atha siṃhaḥ prasenasya śarīrasyāvidūrataḥ || 20 ||
ṛkṣeṇa nihato dṛṣṭaḥ. padair ṛkṣas tu sūcitaḥ. |
padais tair anviyāyātha guhām ṛkṣasya mādhavaḥ. || 21 ||
mahaty ṛkṣabile vāṇīṃ śuśrāva pramaderitāṃ |
dhātryā kumāram ādāya sutaṃ jāmbavato dvijāḥ |
krīḍayantyatha maṇinā mā rodīr ity udīritāṃ. || 22 ||
jāmbavān ṛkṣarājas tu
tāṃ siṃhaṃ nijaghāna vai |
ādāya ca maṇiṃ divyaṃ
svaṃ bilaṃ praviveśa ha. || 15 ||
tat karma kṛṣṇasya tato
vṛṣṇy-andhakamahattarāḥ |
maṇau gṛdhnos tu manvānās
tam eva viśaśaṅkire. || 16 ||
mithyābhīśastiṃ tebhhyas tāṃ
balavān arisūdanaḥ |
amṛṣyamāṇo bhagavān
vanaṃ sa vicacāra ha. || 17 ||
siṃhaḥ prasenam avadhīt, siṃho jāmbavatā hataḥ, |
sukumāraka mā roḍīs, tava hy eṣa syamantakah! || 23 ||
vyaktīkṛtaś ca śabdah sa tūrṇaṃ cāpi bilaṃ yayau |
| [apaśyac ca bilābhyāśe prasenam avadāritaṃ. | ]
praviśya tatra bhagavāṃṣ tad ṛkṣabilam añjasā || 24 ||
sthāpayitvā biladvāre
yadūṃl lāṅgalinā saha |
śārṅgadhanvā bilasthaṃ tu
jāmbavantaṃ dadarśa ha. || 25 ||
dadarśa ṛkṣarājānaṃ
jāmbavantam udāradhīḥ. || 25 ||
yuyudhe vāsudevas tu bile jāmbavatā saha |
bāhubhyām eva govindo divasān ekaviṃśatiṃ. || 26 ||
praviṣṭe tu bilaṃ kṛṣṇe vasudevapuraḥsarāḥ |
punar dvāravatīm etya hataṃ kṛṣṇaṃ nyavedayan. || 27 ||
vāsudevas tu nirjitya jāmbavantaṃ mahābalaṃ |
lebhe jāmbavatīṃ kanyām ṛkṣarājasya saṃmatāṃ. || 28 ||
| bhagavattejasā grasto jāmbavān prasabhaṃ maṇiṃ |
| sutāṃ jāmbavatīm āśu viṣvaksenāya dattavān. || 28 ||
maṇiṃ syamantakaṃ caiva jagrābātmaviśudhaye, |
anuniyarkṣarājaṃ tu niryayau ca tato bilāt. || 29 ||
| upāyād dvārakāṃ kṛṣṇaḥ sa vinā taiḥ puraḥsaraiḥ. |
evaṃ sa maṇim āhṛtya viśodhyātmānam acyutaḥ |
dadau satrājite taṃ vai sarva-sāttvatasamṣadi: || 30 ||
evaṃ mithyābhiśastena
kṛṣṇenaṃitraghātinaṃ |
ātmā viśodhitaḥ pāpād
vinirjitya syamantakaṃ. || 31 ||
| imāṃ mithyābhiśastīṃ yaḥ kṛṣṇasyeha vyapohitāṃ |
| veda, mithyābhiśastīṃ sa nābhisprśati karhicit. || 31 ||
satrājito daśa tv āsan bhāryās, tāsāṃ śatāṃ sutāḥ, |
khyātimantas trayas teṣāṃ: bhaṅgakāras tu pūrvajaḥ || 32 ||
vīro vātapatiś caiva upasvāvāṃs tathaiva ca. |
sadā hi prārthayāmāsa satyabhāmām aninditāṃ |
akrūro 'ntaram anvicchan maṇiṃ caiva syamantakaṃ. || 36 ||
satrājitaṃ tato hatvā śatadhanvā mahābalaḥ |
rātrau taṃ maṇiṃ ādāya tato 'krūrāya dattavān. || 37 ||
akrūras tu tadā ratnam ādāya sa nararṣabhaḥ |
samayaṃ kārāyāṃ cakre: nāvedyo 'haṃ tvayety uta, || 38 ||
vayam abhyupapatsyāmaḥ kṛṣṇena tvāṃ pradharṣitaṃ, |
mamādyā dvārakā sarvā vaśe tiṣṭhaty asaṃśayaṃ. || 39 ||
hate pitari duḥkhārtā satyabhāmā manasvinī |
prayayau ratham āruhya nagaraṃ vāraṇāvataṃ. || 40 ||
satyabhāmā tu tad vṛttaṃ bhojasya śatadhanvanaḥ |
bhartur nirvedya duḥkhārtā pārśvasthaśrūṇy avartayat. || 41 ||
pāṇḍavānāṃ tu dagdhānāṃ hariḥ kṛtvodakakriyāṃ |
kulyārthe cāpi pāṇḍūnāṃ nyayojayata sātyakiṃ. || 42 ||
tatas tvaritam āgamya dvārakāṃ madhusūdanaḥ |
pūrvajaṃ halinaṃ śrīmān idaṃ vacanam abravīt: || 43 ||
hataḥ prasenaḥ siṃhena satrājic chatadhanvanā, |
syamantakas tu madgāmī, tasya prabhur ahaṃ vibho. || 44 ||
tad āroha rathaṃ śīghraṃ bhojaṃ hatvā mahārathaṃ |
syamantako mahābāho asmākaṃ sa bhaviṣyati! || 45 ||
tataḥ pravavṛte yuddhaṃ tumulaṃ bhoja-kṛṣṇayoḥ, |
śatadhanvā tato 'krūrāṃ sarvatodiśam aikṣata. || 46 ||
saṃrabdhau tāv ubhau tatra drṣṭvā bhoja-janārdanau |
śakto 'pi śāṭhyād dhārdikyam akrūro nānvapadyata. || 47 ||
apayāne tato buddhiṃ bhojaś cakre bhayārditaḥ, |
yojanānāṃ śataṃ sāgraṃ hṛdayā pratyapadyata. || 48 ||
vikhyātā hṛdayā nāma śatayojanagāmiṇī |
bhojasya vaḍavā divyā, yayā kṛṣṇam ayodhayat. || 49 ||
kṣīṇāṃ javena hṛdayām adhvanaḥ śatayojane |
drṣṭvā rathasya svāṃ vṛddhiṃ śatadhanvānam ārdayat. || 50 ||
tatas tasyā hayāyās tu śramāt khedāc ca vai dvijāḥ |
kham utpetur atha prāṇāḥ, kṛṣṇo rāmam athābravīt: || 51 ||
tiṣṭheha tvaṃ mahābāho, drṣṭadoṣā hayā mayā, |
padbhyāṃ gatvā hariṣyāmi maṇiratnaṃ syamantakaṃ. || 52 ||
padbhyām eva tato gatvā śatadhanvānam acyutaḥ |
mithilām abhito taṃ vai jaghāna paramāstravit. || 53 ||
syamantakaṃ ca nāpaśyad dhatvā bhojaṃ mahābalaṃ, |
nivṛttaṃ cābravīt kṛṣṇaṃ ratnaṃ dehīti lāṅgalī. || 54 ||
nāstīti kṛṣṇaś covāca, tato rāmo ruṣānvitaḥ |
dhikśabdapūrvam asakṛt pratyuvāca janārdanam: || 55 ||
bhrātṛtvān marṣayāmy eṣa, svasti te 'stu, vrajāmy ahaṃ, |
kṛtyam̃ na me dvārakayā na tvayā na ca vṛṣṇibhiḥ! || 56 ||
praviveśa tato rāmo mithilām arimardanaḥ |
sarvakāmair upahṛtair mithilenabhipūjitaḥ. || 57 ||
etasminñ eva kāle tu babhrur matimatāṃ varaḥ. |
nānārūpān kratūn sarvān ājahāra nirargalān. || 58 ||
dīkṣām̃ ayam̃ sakavacaṃ rakṣārtham̃ praviveśa ha |
syamantakakṛte prājño gāndīputro mahāyaśāḥ. || 59 ||
atha ratnāni cānyāni dhanāni vividhāni ca |
ṣaṣṭiṃ varṣāṇi dharmāṭmā yajñeṣv eva nyayojayat. || 60 ||
akrūrayajñā iti te khyātās tasya mahātmanaḥ |
bahvannadakṣiṇāḥ sarve sarvakāmapradāyinaḥ. || 61 ||
atha duryodhano rājā gatvā sa mithilāṃ prabhuḥ |
gadāśikṣām̃ tato divyām̃ balabhadrād avāptavān. || 62 ||
prasādyā tu tato rāmo vṛṣṇy-andhaka-mahārathaiḥ |
ānīto dvārakām̃ eva kṛṣṇena ca mahātmanā. || 63 ||
akrūras tv andhakaiḥ sārḍham̃ apāyāt puruṣarṣabhaḥ |
hatvā satrājitam̃ yuddhe | yuddhe hatvā tu śatrughnaṃ
sahabandhuṃ mahābalaḥ. || 64 || | saha bandhumatā balī || 64 ||
| svaphalkatanayāyām̃ tu narāyām̃ narasattām̃au |
| bhaṅgakāra-ya tanayau viśrutau sumahābalau || 64 ||
| jajñāte 'ndhaka-mukhyasya śatrughno bandhumāṃś ca tau. |
| vadhe ca bhaṅgakārasya kṛṣṇo na prītīmān abhūt. || 64 ||
jñātibhedabhayāt kṛṣṇas tam̃ upekṣitavāṃṣ tathā. |
apayāte tath-akrūre nāvarṣat pākaśāsanaḥ, || 65 ||
anāvṛṣṭyā tadā rāṣṭram abhavad bahudhā kṛśaṃ. |
tataḥ prasādayāmāsur akrūrāṃ kukur-āndhakāḥ. || 66 ||
punar dvāravatīṃ prāpte tasmin dānapatau tataḥ |
pravavarṣa sahasrākṣaḥ kakṣe jalanidhes tataḥ. || 67 ||
kanyāṃ cā vāsudevāya svasāraṃ śīlasaṃmatāṃ |
akrūrāḥ pradadau śrīmān prītyarthaṃ yadupuṃgavaḥ. || 68 ||
atha vijñāya yogena kṛṣṇo babhrugatāṃ maṇiṃ |
sabhāmadhyagatāṃ prāha tam akrūrāṃ janārdanaḥ: || 69 ||
yat tad ratnaṃ maṇivaraṃ tava hastagatāṃ vibho, |
tat prayacchasva mānārha, mayi mānāryakaṃ kṛthāḥ! || 70 ||
ṣaṣṭivarṣagate kāle yad roṣo 'bhūt tadā mama |
sa saṃrūḍho 'sakṛt prāptas tadā kālātyayo mahān. || 71 ||
tataḥ kṛṣṇasya vacanāt sarva-sāttvatasaṃsadi |
pradadau taṃ maṇiṃ babhrur akleśena mahāmatiḥ. || 72 ||
tatas tam ārjavaprāptaṃ babhror hastād ariṃdamaḥ |
dadau hṛṣṭamanāḥ kṛṣṇas taṃ maṇiṃ babhrave punaḥ. || 73 ||
sa kṛṣṇahastāt saṃprāpya maṇiratnaṃ syamantakaṃ |
ābadhya gāndinīputro virarājāṃśumān iva. || 74 ||
imāṃ mithyābhiśastiṃ yaḥ | imāṃ mithyābhiśastiṃ yo
kṛṣṇasya samudāhṛtāṃ | viśuddhīṃ api cottamāṃ |
veda, mithyābhiśāpās taṃ
na sprśanti kadācana. || 75 ||
veda, mithyābhiśastiṃ sa
na labheta kathaṃcana. || 75 ||
anamitrāc chinir jajñe kaniṣṭhād vṛṣṇinandanāt, |
śaineyaḥ satyakas, tasmād
yuyudhānāś ca sātyakiḥ. || 1 ||
mādryāḥ putrau tu jajñāte
śrutau vṛṣṇy-andhakāv ubhau,
jajñāte tanayau vṛṣṇeḥ śvaphalkaś citrakas tathā. || 2 ||
satyavāk satyasampannaḥ
satyakas tasya cātmajaḥ, || 1 ||
sātyakir yuyudhānāś ca,
tasya bhūtiḥ suto 'bhavat, |
bhūter yugamḍharaḥ putra
iti bhautyaḥ prakīrtitaḥ. || 1 ||
mādryāḥ sutasya jajñe tu
suto vṛṣṇir yudhājitaḥ, |
citrakas tathā. || 2 ||
śvaphalkas tu mahārājo dharmātmā yatra vartate, |
nāsti vyādhibhayaṃ tatra na cāvṛṣṭibhayaṃ tathā. || 3 ||
kadācit kāśirājasya vibhos tu dvijasattamāḥ |
trīṇi varṣāṇi viṣaye nāvarṣat pākaśāsanaḥ. || 4 ||
sa tatra vāsayāmāsa śvaphalkaṃ paramārcitaṃ, |
śvaphalkaparivartena | śvaphalkaparivāsena
vavarṣa harivāhanaḥ. || 5 || | pravarṣat pākaśāsanaḥ. || 5 ||

gāndinīṃ nāma, gāṃ sā hi dadau viprāya nityaśaḥ. || 6 ||
sā mātur udarasthā vai bahūn varṣagaṇān kila |
nivasantī na vai jajñe, garbhasthāṃ tāṃ pitābravīt: || 7 ||
jāyasva śīghraṃ, bhadraṃ te, kimarthaṃ vāpi tiṣṭhasi? |
provāca cainaṃ garbhasthā sā kanyā: gāṃ dine dine || 8 ||
